# Richer 3D (per-day moods, a lived-in attic, a paper family, and three levels) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give each invitation day its own lit 3D mood (Saturday's moonlit night, Sunday's golden dusk, each in Claro and Oscuro), fill the attic with shared and per-mood props, redraw the family as faceless paper pop-ups in the cats' ink style, and show 3D to almost every guest through three levels (the full book, cards over the live attic, and a paper diorama).

**Architecture:**

- **Logic first.** New pure modules decide everything that can be tested without WebGL: `moodFor` (the look), `roomLevel` and `wantsBackdrop` (the three levels), `deviceTier` and `tierSettings` (low or normal), the lighting values, the sky and star field, the prop factories and their draw-call counts, the family drawing, its texture sheet, its walk, and level 2's camera view.
- **The World chunk stays lazy.** Everything that imports three stays under `src/world/` and loads through `loadWorld()`. Level 2's scene (`Backdrop`) is a named export of the same chunk, so a guest who times out fetches nothing new. The paper diorama and the CSS family are small DOM components in the entry chunk.
- **Colors keep one source.** The four looks live in `tuning.world.looks` as `ColorMix` values. The 3D attic builds `THREE.Color`s from them (`palette.ts`), and `src/headMeta.ts` writes the same mixes as CSS variables for the diorama, under `:root[data-theme=…] [data-mood=…]`.

**Tech Stack:** Vite 8.3, React 19.2, TypeScript 6.0 (strict), Tailwind 4.3, three 0.186, @react-three/fiber 9.7, @react-three/drei 10.7, Vitest 5 with jsdom 30 and Testing Library. No new dependencies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-27-richer-3d-design.md` (this work), with `docs/requirements.md` for everything it does not change.

**About the art:** the props, the diorama, and the family are drawn in code and judged by looking at them. Every shape below is complete, compiling code with starting values in `src/tuning.ts`; the screenshot steps say what to check, and changing a placement or a color afterwards is a tuning change, not a code change.

## Global Constraints

- **Environment:** every shell starts with `export NVM_DIR="$HOME/.nvm"; . "$NVM_DIR/nvm.sh"; nvm use 26.9.0 >/dev/null`. Every Run line below includes it.
- **Tests:** one file with `npx vitest run <file>`; the full suite with `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=2` (the machine is loaded; `npm test` is the same suite). Checks: `npx eslint .`, `npx tsc -b`, `npm run build`.
- **Copy:** every guest-facing string comes from `src/content.ts`. This work adds no new guest-facing copy.
- **Numbers and colors:** every number and color comes from `src/tuning.ts`. There are no hex values or literal colors in components or CSS files. Colors reach CSS only as variables generated by `src/headMeta.ts` (or set inline from tuning). A new hex value is allowed only in `tuning.ts`, with a comment saying why; this plan adds none (the looks are mixes of the six palette tokens and the seven feeling colors).
- **CSS variables:** `src/cssVariables.test.ts` fails if any CSS or component reads an undefined `var(--x)`. Every new variable is defined in the head styles, a stylesheet, or inline.
- **New CSS goes in new files:** `src/styles/diorama.css` and `src/styles/family.css`, imported in `src/index.css` next to `pages.css`. `src/index.css` and `src/styles/pages.css` only lose rules.
- **Lint:** eslint-plugin-react-hooks 7 compiler rules: no ref access during render, no setState synchronously in effects, no impure calls (`Math.random`, `Date.now`, `performance.now`) in render. `npx eslint .` and `npx tsc -b` stay clean after every task.
- **Three.js patterns (as in `src/world/*`):** objects are created once with `useState(create…)`, disposed in `useEffect(() => () => dispose…(x), [x])`, retinted in `useLayoutEffect`, and animated in `useFrame` by mutating refs, materials, or instance matrices, scaled by frame delta, with scratch objects allocated once at module scope. No React state changes per frame.
- **Loading:** the World chunk stays lazy and separate. The entry chunk must not import three.
- **Lights:** at most one shadow-casting light (the door candle). No new lights; lanterns, fireflies, stars, the lamp, and the beams glow by unlit or emissive color only. No post-processing. The pixel-ratio cap stays `tuning.world.maxDpr` (1.75) for normal devices.
- **Budget (spec §8):** at most 100 draw calls per frame in the full book and 60 at level 2.
- **Behavior that stays:** facts are never gated, nobody loses, the gate is unchanged, storage keys are unchanged (`mia-cats`, `mia-theme`, `mia-music`, `mia-progress`, `mia-gate-best`), there are no analytics, pinch zoom works, and focusing a control brings it into view.
- **Git:** work on branch `richer-3d`. Never push. Never touch `main`. Never commit `ideas/` or `src/assets/qr.png`. Commit with this form, the trailer after a blank line:

  ```bash
  git commit -m "$(cat <<'EOF'
  <subject>

  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  EOF
  )"
  ```

## Review Focus

1. **A slow phone at the load timeout.** The flat room opens on the aimed page over the paper diorama; when the World chunk arrives, the live attic fades in behind the cards. The cards do not move, focus is not lost, and the pan position is kept. *Test in Task 7.*
2. **A theme switch mid-visit at level 1 or level 2.** The light and dark looks swap in place: the world or the live attic is not remounted, the cover does not flash back, and the diorama is the same element, recolored by its CSS variables. *Tests in Task 2 (level 1) and Task 7 (level 2 and the diorama).*
3. **Turning the phone at level 2.** The live attic stays mounted and re-frames for the new shape; the flat room's card scale and pan are exactly what the flat room computes on its own. *Test in Task 7.*
4. **The battle or Tus gatos open over level 1 or 2, or the tab hidden.** The book and the live attic stop drawing, and resume when the dialog closes or the tab returns. *Tests in Task 7.*
5. **A low-tier device.** No shadow map, a lower pixel ratio, about half the stars, fireflies, and dust, still within budget, and every fact on the page. *Test in Task 8.*

"Props never cover a page at any station or glide" is pinned by Task 3's placement test (extended in Task 4).

### Task 1: Moods, levels, and tiers: logic and tuning

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/tuning.ts` (the `WorldLook` interface at lines 18–40; `tuning.world.night`/`day` at lines 426–465 become `tuning.world.looks`; `attic.moon` becomes `attic.orb`; new `MoodId`, `TierSettings`, `tuning.world.tiers`, `backdropMaxDpr`, `backdropFadeMs`, `tuning.diorama`)
- Create: `src/logic/mood.ts`, `src/logic/mood.test.ts`, `src/logic/deviceTier.ts`, `src/logic/deviceTier.test.ts`, `src/world/palette.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/logic/book.ts`, `src/logic/book.test.ts`, `src/world/palette.ts`, `src/world/World.tsx`, `src/world/StageLighting.tsx:55-64`, `src/world/Attic.tsx:66-73,97,113-114`, `src/components/WorldRoom.tsx`, `src/headMeta.ts`, `src/headMeta.test.ts`, `src/cssVariables.test.ts`, `src/components/RoomCamera.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- **Consumes:** `DayId` from `src/content.ts`; `ThemeName`, `ColorMix`, `cssColor`, `tokens`, `feelingColors` from `src/tuning.ts`; `fitsBook`, `BookEnvironment` from `src/logic/book.ts`.
- **Produces:**
  - `type MoodId = 'saturday' | 'sunday'` (exported from `src/tuning.ts` and re-exported from `src/logic/mood.ts`).
  - `interface WorldLook` with `fog, wood, floor, beam, skyTop, skyBottom, orb, halo, cloth, key, ambientSky, ambientGround, shaft, sparkle, dust: ColorMix`; `orbKind: 'moon' | 'sun'`; `orbAt: readonly [x: number, y: number]`; `orbSize, keyIntensity, ambient, shaftOpacity, stars, lanternGlow, lampGlow, sketchLightness, warmth: number`; `motes: 'dust' | 'fireflies'`.
  - `tuning.world.looks: Record<MoodId, Record<ThemeName, WorldLook>>`.
  - `interface TierSettings { maxDpr: number; backdropMaxDpr: number; shadows: boolean; countScale: number; motionScale: number }`.
  - `tuning.world.tiers = { lowCores, lowMemoryGb, low: TierSettings }`, `tuning.world.backdropMaxDpr`, `tuning.world.backdropFadeMs`, `tuning.world.attic.orb = { radius, halo, sunHalo, haloOpacity }`, `tuning.diorama = { windowRadius, travelPx, rates, twinkleMs, twinkleStaggerMs, swayMs }`.
  - `moodOfDay: Record<DayId, MoodId>`; `interface MoodLook { mood: MoodId; theme: ThemeName; look: WorldLook }`; `moodFor(day: DayId, theme: ThemeName): MoodLook`.
  - `type DeviceTier = 'low' | 'normal'`; `interface TierNavigator { hardwareConcurrency?: number; deviceMemory?: number }`; `deviceTier(nav: TierNavigator): DeviceTier`; `tierSettings(tier: DeviceTier): TierSettings`; `tierCount(count: number, tier: DeviceTier): number`.
  - `type RoomLevel = 'book' | 'backdrop' | 'diorama'`; `interface RoomEnvironment extends BookEnvironment { bookGaveWay: boolean; failed: boolean; backdropReady: boolean }`; `roomLevel(env: RoomEnvironment): RoomLevel`; `wantsBackdrop(env: Omit<RoomEnvironment, 'backdropReady'>): boolean`.
  - `interface WorldPalette` (every `WorldLook` color as `THREE.Color`, the numbers and kinds as-is, plus `paper: { sketch, bone, warm }`); `colorFrom(spec: ColorMix): THREE.Color`; `palettes: Record<MoodId, Record<ThemeName, WorldPalette>>`; `paletteFor(mood: MoodId, theme: ThemeName): WorldPalette`.
  - `dioramaCss(): string` in `src/headMeta.ts`, emitting `--diorama-sky-top`, `--diorama-sky-bottom`, `--diorama-orb`, `--diorama-halo`, `--diorama-orb-dx`, `--diorama-orb-dy`, `--diorama-orb-scale`, `--diorama-wood`, `--diorama-floor`, `--diorama-beam`, `--diorama-cloth`, `--diorama-fog`, `--diorama-shaft`, `--diorama-shaft-opacity`, `--diorama-sparkle`, `--diorama-stars`, `--diorama-lantern-glow` under `:root[data-theme="<theme>"] [data-mood="<mood>"]`.
  - `WorldProps.mood: MoodId` (World selects `paletteFor(mood, theme)`).

Create `src/logic/mood.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { DayId } from '../content'
import { tuning, type MoodId, type ThemeName } from '../tuning'
import { moodFor, moodOfDay } from './mood'

describe('moodFor (spec §3)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['sat', 'dark', 'saturday'],
    ['sat', 'light', 'saturday'],
    ['sun', 'dark', 'sunday'],
    ['sun', 'light', 'sunday'],
  ] as [DayId, ThemeName, MoodId][])('gives %s in %s the %s look', (day, theme, mood) => {
    const result = moodFor(day, theme)
    expect(result.mood).toBe(mood)
    expect(result.theme).toBe(theme)
    expect(result.look).toBe(tuning.world.looks[mood][theme])
  })

  it('takes the mood from the invite link only: Saturday is night, Sunday is dusk', () => {
    expect(moodOfDay).toEqual({ sat: 'saturday', sun: 'sunday' })
  })

  it('puts the moon in both Saturday looks and the sun in both Sunday looks', () => {
    for (const theme of ['dark', 'light'] as const) {
      expect(moodFor('sat', theme).look.orbKind).toBe('moon')
      expect(moodFor('sun', theme).look.orbKind).toBe('sun')
    }
  })

  it('shows the window stars on Saturday only, and fireflies only at Sunday dusk', () => {
    expect(moodFor('sat', 'dark').look.stars).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(moodFor('sat', 'light').look.stars).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(moodFor('sun', 'dark').look.stars).toBe(0)
    expect(moodFor('sun', 'light').look.stars).toBe(0)
    expect(moodFor('sun', 'dark').look.motes).toBe('fireflies')
    for (const [day, theme] of [['sat', 'dark'], ['sat', 'light'], ['sun', 'light']] as [DayId, ThemeName][]) {
      expect(moodFor(day, theme).look.motes).toBe('dust')
    }
  })

  it('lights the crooked lamp in the light looks and leaves it dark in the dark looks (spec §5)', () => {
    expect(moodFor('sat', 'dark').look.lampGlow).toBe(0)
    expect(moodFor('sun', 'dark').look.lampGlow).toBe(0)
    expect(moodFor('sat', 'light').look.lampGlow).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(moodFor('sun', 'light').look.lampGlow).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })
})
```

Create `src/logic/deviceTier.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'
import { deviceTier, tierCount, tierSettings } from './deviceTier'

const { lowCores, lowMemoryGb } = tuning.world.tiers

describe('deviceTier (spec §8)', () => {
  it('is normal with plenty of cores and memory', () => {
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: 8, deviceMemory: 8 })).toBe('normal')
  })

  it('is low at the core threshold or below, and normal just above it', () => {
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: lowCores, deviceMemory: 8 })).toBe('low')
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: lowCores + 1, deviceMemory: 8 })).toBe('normal')
  })

  it('is low at the memory threshold or below, and normal just above it', () => {
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: 8, deviceMemory: lowMemoryGb })).toBe('low')
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: 8, deviceMemory: lowMemoryGb * 2 })).toBe('normal')
  })

  it('is normal when the browser reports neither (Safari and Firefox have no deviceMemory)', () => {
    expect(deviceTier({})).toBe('normal')
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: 8 })).toBe('normal')
  })

  it('never drops to low on the core count alone when memory is unreported (Safari on iPhone)', () => {
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: lowCores })).toBe('normal')
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: 1 })).toBe('normal')
  })

  it('ignores zero and junk values', () => {
    expect(deviceTier({ hardwareConcurrency: 0, deviceMemory: Number.NaN })).toBe('normal')
  })
})

describe('tierSettings (spec §8)', () => {
  it('turns the shadow map off, caps the pixel ratio, halves the counts, and slows motion on low', () => {
    expect(tierSettings('low')).toEqual({ maxDpr: 1.25, backdropMaxDpr: 1, shadows: false, countScale: 0.5, motionScale: tuning.world.tiers.low.motionScale })
    expect(tierSettings('low').motionScale).toBeLessThan(1)
  })

  it('keeps the design values on normal', () => {
    expect(tierSettings('normal')).toEqual({
      maxDpr: tuning.world.maxDpr,
      backdropMaxDpr: tuning.world.backdropMaxDpr,
      shadows: true,
      countScale: 1,
      motionScale: 1,
    })
  })

  it('draws about half of a count on low, and never none', () => {
    expect(tierCount(40, 'normal')).toBe(40)
    expect(tierCount(40, 'low')).toBe(20)
    expect(tierCount(1, 'low')).toBe(1)
  })
})
```

```ts
describe('three levels (spec §7)', () => {
  const env = { reducedMotion: false, height: 844, webgl: () => true, bookGaveWay: false, failed: false, backdropReady: false }

  it('gives the full book to a tall screen with motion allowed and WebGL', () => {
    expect(roomLevel(env)).toBe('book')
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, height: tall })).toBe('book')
  })

  it('gives the diorama with reduced motion, without asking for WebGL', () => {
    const webgl = vi.fn(() => true)
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, reducedMotion: true, webgl, backdropReady: true })).toBe('diorama')
    expect(webgl).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('gives the diorama without WebGL', () => {
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, webgl: () => false, backdropReady: true })).toBe('diorama')
  })

  it('gives a short screen the live attic once it has arrived, and the diorama until then', () => {
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, height: 390 })).toBe('diorama')
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, height: 390, backdropReady: true })).toBe('backdrop')
  })

  it('gives the live attic after the book timed out or turned too short, once it has arrived', () => {
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, bookGaveWay: true })).toBe('diorama')
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, bookGaveWay: true, backdropReady: true })).toBe('backdrop')
  })

  it('gives the diorama for the rest of the visit after a lost context or an error', () => {
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, failed: true, backdropReady: true })).toBe('diorama')
    expect(roomLevel({ ...env, height: 390, failed: true, backdropReady: true })).toBe('diorama')
  })

  it('loads the live attic only where it can show', () => {
    const start = { reducedMotion: false, height: 390, webgl: () => true, bookGaveWay: false, failed: false }
    expect(wantsBackdrop(start)).toBe(true)
    expect(wantsBackdrop({ ...start, height: 844 })).toBe(false)
    expect(wantsBackdrop({ ...start, height: 844, bookGaveWay: true })).toBe(true)
    expect(wantsBackdrop({ ...start, failed: true })).toBe(false)
    expect(wantsBackdrop({ ...start, reducedMotion: true })).toBe(false)
    expect(wantsBackdrop({ ...start, webgl: () => false })).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing palette and head-style tests.**

Create `src/world/palette.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tokens, tuning, type ColorMix, type MoodId, type ThemeName } from '../tuning'
import { colorFrom, paletteFor, palettes } from './palette'

const looks: [MoodId, ThemeName][] = [
  ['saturday', 'dark'],
  ['saturday', 'light'],
  ['sunday', 'dark'],
  ['sunday', 'light'],
]

// The mix as the tuning module means it, written out independently: from one color toward another.
function expected(spec: ColorMix): THREE.Color {
  if (typeof spec === 'string') return new THREE.Color(spec)
  return new THREE.Color(spec[0]).lerp(new THREE.Color(spec[1]), spec[2])
}

describe('the four looks (spec §3)', () => {
  it.each(looks)('builds %s %s from its tuning look', (mood, theme) => {
    const look = tuning.world.looks[mood][theme]
    const palette = paletteFor(mood, theme)
    expect(palette).toBe(palettes[mood][theme])
    for (const key of ['fog', 'wood', 'floor', 'beam', 'skyTop', 'skyBottom', 'orb', 'halo', 'cloth', 'key', 'ambientSky', 'ambientGround', 'shaft', 'sparkle', 'dust'] as const) {
      expect(palette[key].equals(expected(look[key])), key).toBe(true)
    }
    expect(palette.keyIntensity).toBe(look.keyIntensity)
    expect(palette.ambient).toBe(look.ambient)
    expect(palette.orbKind).toBe(look.orbKind)
    expect(palette.orbAt).toEqual(look.orbAt)
    expect(palette.motes).toBe(look.motes)
  })

  it('gives each of the four looks its own sky', () => {
    const skies = new Set(looks.map(([mood, theme]) => paletteFor(mood, theme).skyTop.getHexString()))
    expect(skies.size).toBe(4)
  })

  it('mixes a color from one token toward another', () => {
    expect(colorFrom([tokens.ink, tokens.bone, 0.25]).equals(expected([tokens.ink, tokens.bone, 0.25]))).toBe(true)
    expect(colorFrom(tokens.rose).equals(new THREE.Color(tokens.rose))).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

```ts
describe('dioramaCss (spec §7 Level 3)', () => {
  const css = dioramaCss()

  it('emits one block of colors per mood and theme, from the attic looks', () => {
    for (const mood of ['saturday', 'sunday'] as const) {
      for (const theme of ['dark', 'light'] as const) {
        const look = tuning.world.looks[mood][theme]
        expect(css).toContain(
          `:root[data-theme="${theme}"] [data-mood="${mood}"]{--diorama-sky-top:${cssColor(look.skyTop)};--diorama-sky-bottom:${cssColor(look.skyBottom)};`,
        )
        expect(css).toContain(`--diorama-orb:${cssColor(look.orb)};`)
      }
    }
  })

  it('places the moon or the sun where the 3D window has it, in the diorama window units', () => {
    const look = tuning.world.looks.saturday.dark
    const { windowRadius } = tuning.world.attic
    const dx = Number(((look.orbAt[0] / windowRadius) * tuning.diorama.windowRadius).toFixed(2))
    expect(css).toContain(`--diorama-orb-dx:${dx}px`)
  })

  it('is part of the head styles', () => {
    expect(headMeta(siteUrl)).toContain(dioramaCss())
  })

  it('uses only the palette tokens and the feeling colors (no new colors)', () => {
    const allowed = new Set([...Object.values(tokens), ...Object.values(feelingColors)].map((c) => c.toUpperCase()))
    for (const hex of css.match(/#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}/g) ?? []) expect(allowed.has(hex.toUpperCase()), hex).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

```ts
  default: function World({
    ids,

  }: {
    ids: LandmarkId[]

  },
}))
```

```ts
  it("hands the day's mood to the world: Saturday's night, Sunday's dusk (spec §3)", async () => {
    world.mode = 'ready'
    const saturday = renderWorldRoom()
```

```ts
// A color straight from the palette, or a mix: from one token toward another by an amount.
export type ColorMix = string | readonly [from: string, to: string, amount: number]

// Spec §3: the invite link picks the mood. Saturday is a moonlit night, Sunday a golden dusk.
export type MoodId = 'saturday' | 'sunday'

// How the attic looks in one mood and theme (spec §3). Claro is the lighter version of each mood,
// Oscuro the darker. The 3D attic (src/world/palette.ts) and the paper diorama (src/headMeta.ts)
// both read these.
export interface WorldLook {
  fog: ColorMix
  wood: ColorMix
  floor: ColorMix
  // The rafters and the window frame.
  beam: ColorMix
  // The sky behind the round window: a gradient from its top to the horizon.
  skyTop: ColorMix
  skyBottom: ColorMix
  // The moon or the sun in the window: where it sits (world units from the window's centre, y up),
  // its size (times tuning.world.attic.orb.radius), its color, and its halo.
  orbKind: 'moon' | 'sun'
  orbAt: readonly [x: number, y: number]
  orbSize: number
  orb: ColorMix
  halo: ColorMix
  cloth: ColorMix
  // The key light through the window and the ambient light from above and below. The attic is lit
  // at these values on every page (spec §4): only the book's paper and the cats follow the stage.
  key: ColorMix
  keyIntensity: number
  ambientSky: ColorMix
  ambientGround: ColorMix
  ambient: number
  // The beam of light from the window (the moonbeam, or the golden beam): unlit and additive.
  shaft: ColorMix
  shaftOpacity: number
  // The window's stars (Saturday) or the fireflies (Sunday), and how bright the window's stars shine
  // (0: none).
  sparkle: ColorMix
  stars: number
  // What drifts in the air (spec §3 Motion rule): dust, or fireflies at Sunday's dusk.
  motes: 'dust' | 'fireflies'
  // How lit the lanterns (Sunday) and the crooked lamp are, 0 to 1. They glow by their own color only.
  lanternGlow: number
  lampGlow: number
  // Pencil paper is bone with no color at this lightness; candles warm it by `warmth`.
  sketchLightness: number
  warmth: number
  dust: ColorMix
}

// Spec §8 Device tiers: what a tier draws.
export interface TierSettings {
  // The pixel-ratio caps of the book and of level 2's live attic.
  maxDpr: number
  backdropMaxDpr: number
  // The candle's real shadow map.
  shadows: boolean
  // The share of the stars, fireflies, and dust that are drawn.
  countScale: number
  // The speed of the ambient motion (the mobile, the lanterns, the stars, the fireflies).
  motionScale: number
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Add the mood and tier modules.**

Create `src/logic/mood.ts`:

```ts
import type { DayId } from '../content'
import { tuning, type MoodId, type ThemeName, type WorldLook } from '../tuning'

export type { MoodId }

// Spec §3: the invite link picks the mood (Saturday is a moonlit night, Sunday a golden dusk), and
// the theme picks its lighter (Claro) or darker (Oscuro) version. Never the clock: after the party
// the look stays the same.
export const moodOfDay: Record<DayId, MoodId> = { sat: 'saturday', sun: 'sunday' }

export interface MoodLook {
  mood: MoodId
  theme: ThemeName
  look: WorldLook
}

export function moodFor(day: DayId, theme: ThemeName): MoodLook {
  const mood = moodOfDay[day]
  return { mood, theme, look: tuning.world.looks[mood][theme] }
}
```

Create `src/logic/deviceTier.ts`:

```ts
import { tuning, type TierSettings } from '../tuning'

export type DeviceTier = 'low' | 'normal'

// What deviceTier reads: navigator.hardwareConcurrency, and navigator.deviceMemory where the browser
// has it (Chromium only).
export interface TierNavigator {
  hardwareConcurrency?: number
  deviceMemory?: number
}

const known = (value: number | undefined): value is number =>
  typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0

// Spec §8 Device tiers: little memory, or few cores, make a low device. Only Chromium reports
// deviceMemory, and there both numbers are honest. Safari and Firefox report no memory, and Safari's
// core count is not a reliable signal, so without a memory reading the device counts as normal
// (an iPhone must never drop to low on its core count alone).
export function deviceTier(nav: TierNavigator): DeviceTier {
  const { lowCores, lowMemoryGb } = tuning.world.tiers
  const cores = nav.hardwareConcurrency
  const memory = nav.deviceMemory
  if (!known(memory)) return 'normal'
  if (memory <= lowMemoryGb) return 'low'
  if (known(cores) && cores <= lowCores) return 'low'
  return 'normal'
}

export function tierSettings(tier: DeviceTier): TierSettings {
  if (tier === 'low') return tuning.world.tiers.low
  return {
    maxDpr: tuning.world.maxDpr,
    backdropMaxDpr: tuning.world.backdropMaxDpr,
    shadows: true,
    countScale: 1,
    motionScale: 1,
  }
}

// How many stars, fireflies, or dust motes a tier draws: about half on low, and at least one.
export function tierCount(count: number, tier: DeviceTier): number {
  return Math.max(1, Math.round(count * tierSettings(tier).countScale))
}
```

In `src/logic/book.ts`, add after `wantsBook`:

```ts
// Spec §7: the full book, the flat room's cards over the live 3D attic (the backdrop), or over the
// paper diorama.
export type RoomLevel = 'book' | 'backdrop' | 'diorama'

export interface RoomEnvironment extends BookEnvironment {
  // The book gave way this visit: it did not get ready within loadTimeoutMs, or the screen turned
  // too short. The live attic may follow.
  bookGaveWay: boolean
  // The 3D failed this visit: the WebGL context was lost, the renderer could not start, an error was
  // caught, or the chunk failed to load. No retry.
  failed: boolean
  // Level 2's scene has arrived and drawn its first frame.
  backdropReady: boolean
}

export function roomLevel(env: RoomEnvironment): RoomLevel {
  if (env.reducedMotion || env.failed || !env.webgl()) return 'diorama'
  if (fitsBook(env.height) && !env.bookGaveWay) return 'book'
  return env.backdropReady ? 'backdrop' : 'diorama'
}

// Whether the flat room loads the live attic behind its cards (it shows once it is ready).
export function wantsBackdrop(env: Omit<RoomEnvironment, 'backdropReady'>): boolean {
  return roomLevel({ ...env, backdropReady: true }) === 'backdrop'
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Build the four palettes and update their callers.**

Replace `src/world/palette.ts` with:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { tokens, tuning, type ColorMix, type MoodId, type ThemeName, type WorldLook } from '../tuning'

// Spec §3: two moods, one attic. Each mood and theme's look, built once from the palette tokens as
// ready colors, so switching theme only copies colors.
export interface WorldPalette {
  fog: THREE.Color
  wood: THREE.Color
  floor: THREE.Color
  beam: THREE.Color
  skyTop: THREE.Color
  skyBottom: THREE.Color
  orbKind: 'moon' | 'sun'
  orbAt: readonly [number, number]
  orbSize: number
  orb: THREE.Color
  halo: THREE.Color
  cloth: THREE.Color
  key: THREE.Color
  keyIntensity: number
  ambientSky: THREE.Color
  ambientGround: THREE.Color
  ambient: number
  shaft: THREE.Color
  shaftOpacity: number
  sparkle: THREE.Color
  stars: number
  motes: 'dust' | 'fireflies'
  lanternGlow: number
  lampGlow: number
  // The book's paper tint for each stage: gray pencil paper, bone parchment, and bone warmed by candles.
  paper: { sketch: THREE.Color; bone: THREE.Color; warm: THREE.Color }
  dust: THREE.Color
}

export function colorFrom(spec: ColorMix): THREE.Color {
  if (typeof spec === 'string') return new THREE.Color(spec)
  const [from, to, amount] = spec
  return new THREE.Color(from).lerp(new THREE.Color(to), amount)
}

function build(look: WorldLook): WorldPalette {
  const bone = new THREE.Color(tokens.bone)
  const hsl = { h: 0, s: 0, l: 0 }
  bone.getHSL(hsl)
  return {
    fog: colorFrom(look.fog),
    wood: colorFrom(look.wood),
    floor: colorFrom(look.floor),
    beam: colorFrom(look.beam),
    skyTop: colorFrom(look.skyTop),
    skyBottom: colorFrom(look.skyBottom),
    orbKind: look.orbKind,
    orbAt: look.orbAt,
    orbSize: look.orbSize,
    orb: colorFrom(look.orb),
    halo: colorFrom(look.halo),
    cloth: colorFrom(look.cloth),
    key: colorFrom(look.key),
    keyIntensity: look.keyIntensity,
    ambientSky: colorFrom(look.ambientSky),
    ambientGround: colorFrom(look.ambientGround),
    ambient: look.ambient,
    shaft: colorFrom(look.shaft),
    shaftOpacity: look.shaftOpacity,
    sparkle: colorFrom(look.sparkle),
    stars: look.stars,
    motes: look.motes,
    lanternGlow: look.lanternGlow,
    lampGlow: look.lampGlow,
    paper: {
      sketch: new THREE.Color().setHSL(hsl.h, 0, hsl.l * look.sketchLightness),
      bone,
      warm: bone.clone().lerp(new THREE.Color(tokens.candle), look.warmth),
    },
    dust: colorFrom(look.dust),
  }
}

const { looks } = tuning.world

export const palettes: Record<MoodId, Record<ThemeName, WorldPalette>> = {
  saturday: { dark: build(looks.saturday.dark), light: build(looks.saturday.light) },
  sunday: { dark: build(looks.sunday.dark), light: build(looks.sunday.light) },
}

export function paletteFor(mood: MoodId, theme: ThemeName): WorldPalette {
  return palettes[mood][theme]
}

// Sets a material's color from the palette. Kept outside components, so retinting a shared
// material is one plain call.
export function paint(material: { color: THREE.Color }, next: THREE.Color) {
  material.color.copy(next)
}
```

add after `screenCss()`:

```ts
const round2 = (value: number) => Number(value.toFixed(2))

// Spec §7 Level 3: the paper diorama's colors, one set per mood and theme, from the same looks as
// the 3D attic (tuning.world.looks). The diorama carries data-mood and the root carries data-theme,
// so switching theme recolors it in place. The moon or sun sits where the 3D window has it, in the
// diorama window's units.
function dioramaVars(look: WorldLook): string {
  const { windowRadius, orb } = tuning.world.attic
  const toDiorama = tuning.diorama.windowRadius / windowRadius
  return [
    `--diorama-sky-top:${cssColor(look.skyTop)}`,
    `--diorama-sky-bottom:${cssColor(look.skyBottom)}`,
    `--diorama-orb:${cssColor(look.orb)}`,
    `--diorama-halo:${cssColor(look.halo)}`,
    `--diorama-orb-dx:${round2(look.orbAt[0] * toDiorama)}px`,
    `--diorama-orb-dy:${round2(-look.orbAt[1] * toDiorama)}px`,
    `--diorama-orb-scale:${round2((orb.radius * look.orbSize) / windowRadius)}`,
    `--diorama-wood:${cssColor(look.wood)}`,
    `--diorama-floor:${cssColor(look.floor)}`,
    `--diorama-beam:${cssColor(look.beam)}`,
    `--diorama-cloth:${cssColor(look.cloth)}`,
    `--diorama-fog:${cssColor(look.fog)}`,
    `--diorama-shaft:${cssColor(look.shaft)}`,
    `--diorama-shaft-opacity:${look.shaftOpacity}`,
    `--diorama-sparkle:${cssColor(look.sparkle)}`,
    `--diorama-stars:${look.stars}`,
    `--diorama-lantern-glow:${look.lanternGlow}`,
  ].join(';')
}

export function dioramaCss(): string {
  const moods = Object.keys(tuning.world.looks) as MoodId[]
  const names = Object.keys(themes) as ThemeName[]
  return moods
    .flatMap((mood) =>
      names.map((theme) => `:root[data-theme="${theme}"] [data-mood="${mood}"]{${dioramaVars(tuning.world.looks[mood][theme])}}`),
    )
    .join('')
}
```


```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tuning, type MoodId, type ThemeName } from '../tuning'
import { candleBrightness, candleFlicker, moodLight } from './lighting'
import { paletteFor } from './palette'
import { createStageMix, writeStageMix } from './stageMix'

const at = (progress: number) => writeStageMix(progress, createStageMix())
const looks: [MoodId, ThemeName][] = [
  ['saturday', 'dark'],
  ['saturday', 'light'],
  ['sunday', 'dark'],
  ['sunday', 'light'],
]

describe('lit from the start (spec §4)', () => {
  it.each(looks)('lights the %s %s attic by its mood alone', (mood, theme) => {
    const look = tuning.world.looks[mood][theme]
    expect(moodLight(paletteFor(mood, theme))).toEqual({ keyIntensity: look.keyIntensity, ambientIntensity: look.ambient })
  })

  it('takes nothing from the stage: the palette is its only input', () => {
    expect(moodLight).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('keeps the candles dark until luz, and at full brightness at the door', () => {
    expect(candleBrightness(at(0))).toBe(0)
    expect(candleBrightness(at(0.5))).toBe(0)
    expect(candleBrightness(at(0.8))).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(candleBrightness(at(1))).toBeCloseTo(tuning.world.light.candle, 6)
  })

  it('flickers each candle softly around its brightness, out of step with the others', () => {
    const { flicker } = tuning.world.light
    for (let t = 0; t < 5; t += 0.37) {
      const f = candleFlicker(t, 1)
      expect(f).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1 - flicker)
      expect(f).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1 + flicker)
    }
    expect(candleFlicker(1, 0)).not.toBe(candleFlicker(1, 1))
  })
})
```

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tierCount } from '../logic/deviceTier'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'
import { starField, twinkle, writeSkyColors } from './sky'

describe('the window sky (spec §3)', () => {
  it('runs from the horizon color at the bottom to the top color at the top', () => {
    const plane = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1, 1, 4)
    const positions = plane.attributes.position as THREE.BufferAttribute
    const colors = new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(positions.count * 3), 3)
    writeSkyColors(colors, positions, new THREE.Color(1, 0, 0), new THREE.Color(0, 0, 1))
    // PlaneGeometry lists its rows from the top (y 0.5) to the bottom (y -0.5).
    expect([colors.getX(0), colors.getY(0), colors.getZ(0)]).toEqual([1, 0, 0])
    const last = colors.count - 1
    expect([colors.getX(last), colors.getY(last), colors.getZ(last)]).toEqual([0, 0, 1])
    expect(colors.getX(4)).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(colors.getX(4)).toBeLessThan(1)
    plane.dispose()
  })

  it('scatters the stars inside the window, the same way on every load', () => {
    const a = starField(40, 2)
    const b = starField(40, 2)
    expect(a.count).toBe(40)
    expect(Array.from(a.x)).toEqual(Array.from(b.x))
    for (let i = 0; i < a.count; i++) expect(Math.hypot(a.x[i]!, a.y[i]!)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2)
  })

  it('twinkles each star between its dimmest and its full size', () => {
    const { min } = tuning.world.stars
    const seen = new Set<number>()
    for (let t = 0; t < 6; t += 0.25) {
      const k = twinkle(t, 0.7)
      expect(k).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(min - 1e-9)
      expect(k).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1 + 1e-9)
      seen.add(Math.round(k * 100))
    }
    expect(seen.size).toBeGreaterThan(3)
  })

  it('draws about half the stars on a low-tier device (spec §8)', () => {
    const { count } = tuning.world.stars
    expect(tierCount(count, 'low')).toBe(Math.round(count / 2))
  })
})
```

Create `src/world/lighting.ts`:

```ts
import { tuning } from '../tuning'
import type { WorldPalette } from './palette'
import type { StageMix } from './stageMix'

// Spec §4 Lit from the start: the attic's key light (through the window) and its ambient light are
// the mood's, on every page. Nothing here reads the stage.
export function moodLight(palette: WorldPalette): { keyIntensity: number; ambientIntensity: number } {
  return { keyIntensity: palette.keyIntensity, ambientIntensity: palette.ambient }
}

// Design §1 Evolution, kept for the book only: the candles are dark until luz, then brighten toward
// the family door.
export function candleBrightness(mix: StageMix): number {
  const { candle, candleBase } = tuning.world.light
  return candle * mix.light * (candleBase + (1 - candleBase) * mix.warm)
}

// A candle's soft, uneven flicker at time t: two waves, each candle a phase apart from the next.
export function candleFlicker(t: number, index: number): number {
  const { flicker, flickerWaves } = tuning.world.light
  const { fast, slow, phase } = flickerWaves
  return 1 + flicker * Math.sin(t * fast + index * phase) * Math.sin(t * slow + index)
}
```

Create `src/world/MoodLights.tsx`:

```tsx
import { tuning } from '../tuning'
import { moodLight } from './lighting'
import type { WorldPalette } from './palette'

// Spec §4: the attic is lit by its mood only. The window's key light and the ambient light from above
// and below keep the mood's values on every page; switching theme copies the new colors in place.
export function MoodLights({ palette }: { palette: WorldPalette }) {
  const light = moodLight(palette)
  return (
    <>
      <hemisphereLight color={palette.ambientSky} groundColor={palette.ambientGround} intensity={light.ambientIntensity} />
      <directionalLight color={palette.key} intensity={light.keyIntensity} position={[...tuning.world.light.moonFrom]} />
    </>
  )
}
```

Replace `src/world/StageLighting.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, type RefObject } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { tokens, tuning } from '../tuning'
import type { BookPath } from './bookPath'
import { candleBrightness, candleFlicker } from './lighting'
import { MoodLights } from './MoodLights'
import type { WorldPalette } from './palette'
import { applyStageMix, type WorldResources } from './resources'
import { createStageMix, writeStageMix } from './stageMix'

export interface ShadowState {
  drawn: boolean
  stale: boolean
}

interface Props {
  path: BookPath
  viewRef: RefObject<{ progress: number }>
  resources: WorldResources
  palette: WorldPalette
  // The candle's shadow map: whether it has been drawn yet, and whether a paper piece has moved
  // since (Station marks it stale).
  shadowRef: RefObject<ShadowState>
}

const mix = createStageMix()

// Spec §4 Lit from the start: the attic is lit by its mood on every page (MoodLights). The stage
// (design §1 Evolution) drives only the book: its paper warms, its ink fades in, and in luz the
// candles light (one casts shadows) and warm the paper toward the door.
export function StageLighting({ path, viewRef, resources, palette, shadowRef }: Props) {
  const { candleAt, candleOffset, doorOffset, candleDistance, shadowMapPx } = tuning.world.light
  const candles = useRef<(THREE.PointLight | null)[]>([])

  // Candles stand beside the path; the last one waits behind the family door.
  const spots = useMemo(
    () =>
      candleAt.map((t, i) => {
        const [x, y, z] = i === candleAt.length - 1 ? doorOffset : candleOffset
        return path.ground.getPoint(t).add(new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z))
      }),
    [candleAt, candleOffset, doorOffset, path],
  )

  // A new path (the screen changed shape) moves the candles and the pages: redraw the shadows.
  useEffect(() => {
    shadowRef.current.stale = true
  }, [spots, shadowRef])

  useFrame((state) => {
    writeStageMix(viewRef.current.progress, mix)
    const t = state.clock.elapsedTime
    const lit = candleBrightness(mix)
    for (let i = 0; i < candles.current.length; i++) {
      const light = candles.current[i]
      if (light) light.intensity = lit * candleFlicker(t, i)
    }

    applyStageMix(resources, mix, palette)

    // One candle casts shadows (six passes, a point light's cube). The light stands still and only
    // the paper pieces cast, so the map is redrawn only after a piece has moved, and only while
    // the candles are lit; the first frame draws it so the texture exists. A settled book in luz
    // redraws nothing.
    const shadow = shadowRef.current
    const draw = !shadow.drawn || (shadow.stale && mix.light > 0.001)
    state.gl.shadowMap.needsUpdate = draw
    if (draw) {
      shadow.drawn = true
      shadow.stale = false
    }
  })

  return (
    <>
      <MoodLights palette={palette} />
      {spots.map((spot, i) => {
        const door = i === spots.length - 1
        return (
          <pointLight
            key={i}
            ref={(light) => {
              candles.current[i] = light
            }}
            position={spot}
            color={tokens.candle}
            intensity={0}
            distance={candleDistance}
            decay={2}
            castShadow={door}
            shadow-mapSize={[shadowMapPx, shadowMapPx]}
            shadow-camera-near={0.1}
            shadow-camera-far={candleDistance}
            shadow-bias={-0.003}
          />
        )
      })}
    </>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the sky helpers and the window sky.**

Create `src/world/sky.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'

const scratch = new THREE.Color()

// Spec §3: the sky behind the round window, a gradient from its top down to the horizon. Writes one
// color per vertex of a plane whose y runs from -0.5 (the horizon) to 0.5 (the top).
export function writeSkyColors(
  colors: THREE.BufferAttribute,
  positions: THREE.BufferAttribute,
  top: THREE.Color,
  bottom: THREE.Color,
): void {
  for (let i = 0; i < positions.count; i++) {
    const share = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(positions.getY(i) + 0.5, 0, 1)
    scratch.copy(bottom).lerp(top, share)
    colors.setXYZ(i, scratch.r, scratch.g, scratch.b)
  }
  colors.needsUpdate = true
}

export interface StarField {
  count: number
  x: Float32Array
  y: Float32Array
  size: Float32Array
  phase: Float32Array
}

// Saturday's stars (spec §3): a fixed scatter inside a disc of `radius` (the window), so every load
// looks the same.
export function starField(count: number, radius: number): StarField {
  const { size, sizeRange } = tuning.world.stars
  let seed = 5
  const next = () => {
    seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647
    return seed / 2147483647
  }
  const field: StarField = {
    count,
    x: new Float32Array(count),
    y: new Float32Array(count),
    size: new Float32Array(count),
    phase: new Float32Array(count),
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const r = radius * Math.sqrt(next())
    const a = next() * Math.PI * 2
    field.x[i] = r * Math.cos(a)
    field.y[i] = r * Math.sin(a)
    field.size[i] = size + next() * sizeRange
    field.phase[i] = next() * Math.PI * 2
  }
  return field
}

// A star's twinkle at time t: its size swings between `min` and all of itself.
export function twinkle(t: number, phase: number): number {
  const { min, speed } = tuning.world.stars
  return min + (1 - min) * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * speed + phase))
}
```

Create `src/world/WindowSky.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { tierCount, tierSettings, type DeviceTier } from '../logic/deviceTier'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'
import { paint, type WorldPalette } from './palette'
import { glowTexture } from './paperTextures'
import { starField, twinkle, writeSkyColors } from './sky'

// Spec §3: what the round window shows, per look: the sky as a gradient, the moon or the sun with its
// halo, and on Saturday twinkling stars. It all sits behind the gable wall and shows through the
// window's hole. Nothing here casts or takes light.

function createSky() {
  const plane = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1, 1, 8)
  plane.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(plane.attributes.position!.count * 3), 3))
  const glow = glowTexture()
  return {
    plane,
    disc: new THREE.CircleGeometry(1, 40),
    haloPlane: new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2),
    star: new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1),
    glow,
    sky: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ vertexColors: true, fog: false }),
    orb: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ fog: false }),
    halo: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: glow, transparent: true, depthWrite: false, fog: false }),
    starMaterial: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      map: glow,
      transparent: true,
      depthWrite: false,
      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
      fog: false,
    }),
  }
}

type SkyParts = ReturnType<typeof createSky>

function applySky(parts: SkyParts, palette: WorldPalette) {
  writeSkyColors(
    parts.plane.attributes.color as THREE.BufferAttribute,
    parts.plane.attributes.position as THREE.BufferAttribute,
    palette.skyTop,
    palette.skyBottom,
  )
  paint(parts.orb, palette.orb)
  paint(parts.halo, palette.halo)
  parts.halo.opacity = tuning.world.attic.orb.haloOpacity
  paint(parts.starMaterial, palette.sparkle)
  parts.starMaterial.opacity = palette.stars
}

function disposeSky(parts: SkyParts) {
  for (const geometry of [parts.plane, parts.disc, parts.haloPlane, parts.star]) geometry.dispose()
  for (const material of [parts.sky, parts.orb, parts.halo, parts.starMaterial]) material.dispose()
  parts.glow.dispose()
}

// One scratch object, reused every frame.
const star = new THREE.Object3D()

function Stars({ parts, tier }: { parts: SkyParts; tier: DeviceTier }) {
  const count = tierCount(tuning.world.stars.count, tier)
  const field = useMemo(() => starField(count, tuning.world.attic.windowRadius), [count])
  const mesh = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null)
  const clock = useRef(0)
  const motion = tierSettings(tier).motionScale

  useFrame((_, delta) => {
    const m = mesh.current
    if (!m) return
    clock.current += Math.min(delta, 0.1) * motion
    const t = clock.current
    for (let i = 0; i < field.count; i++) {
      star.position.set(field.x[i]!, field.y[i]!, 0)
      star.scale.setScalar(field.size[i]! * twinkle(t, field.phase[i]!))
      star.updateMatrix()
      m.setMatrixAt(i, star.matrix)
    }
    m.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true
  })

  return <instancedMesh ref={mesh} args={[parts.star, parts.starMaterial, count]} frustumCulled={false} />
}

export function WindowSky({ palette, tier }: { palette: WorldPalette; tier: DeviceTier }) {
  const [parts] = useState(createSky)
  useEffect(() => () => disposeSky(parts), [parts])
  // Switching theme repaints the sky in place.
  useLayoutEffect(() => applySky(parts, palette), [parts, palette])
  const { wallZ, windowY, windowRadius, skyScale, orb } = tuning.world.attic
  const radius = orb.radius * palette.orbSize
  const halo = radius * (palette.orbKind === 'sun' ? orb.sunHalo : orb.halo)
  const [x, y] = palette.orbAt

  return (
    <group position={[0, windowY, wallZ]}>
      <mesh geometry={parts.plane} material={parts.sky} position-z={-0.8} scale={windowRadius * skyScale * 2} />
      {palette.stars > 0 && (
        <group position-z={-0.78}>
          <Stars parts={parts} tier={tier} />
        </group>
      )}
      <mesh geometry={parts.haloPlane} material={parts.halo} position={[x, y, -0.7]} scale={halo} />
      <mesh geometry={parts.disc} material={parts.orb} position={[x, y, -0.6]} scale={radius} />
    </group>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Take the sky out of the attic.** Replace `src/world/Attic.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useState } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'
import { paint, type WorldPalette } from './palette'
import { floorTexture } from './paperTextures'

// Design §1: a cat's attic. Floorboards, a gable wall with a round window, and a slanted roof with
// rafters. What the window shows (the sky, the moon or the sun, the stars) is WindowSky's (spec §3).
// Nothing here moves.

function createAttic() {
  const { wallZ, frontZ, halfWidth, ridge, eave, windowY, windowRadius, roughness } = tuning.world.attic
  const depth = frontZ - wallZ

  // Colors come from the look's palette once mounted (see applyPalette).
  const floorMap = floorTexture()
  floorMap.repeat.set(halfWidth / 3, depth / 3)
  const floor = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorMap, roughness: roughness.floor })
  const wall = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ roughness: roughness.wall, side: THREE.DoubleSide })
  const beam = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ roughness: roughness.beam })

  const gable = new THREE.Shape()
  gable.moveTo(-halfWidth, 0)
  gable.lineTo(halfWidth, 0)
  gable.lineTo(halfWidth, eave)
  gable.lineTo(0, ridge)
  gable.lineTo(-halfWidth, eave)
  gable.closePath()
  const hole = new THREE.Path()
  hole.absarc(0, windowY, windowRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2, false)
  gable.holes.push(hole)

  const slope = Math.hypot(halfWidth, ridge - eave)
  return {
    materials: [floor, wall, beam],
    textures: [floorMap],
    floor,
    wall,
    beam,
    floorGeometry: new THREE.PlaneGeometry(halfWidth * 2, depth),
    gableGeometry: new THREE.ShapeGeometry(gable, 32),
    frameGeometry: new THREE.TorusGeometry(windowRadius, 0.14, 8, 48),
    barGeometry: new THREE.BoxGeometry(windowRadius * 2, 0.1, 0.12),
    roofGeometry: new THREE.PlaneGeometry(slope, depth),
    kneeGeometry: new THREE.PlaneGeometry(depth, eave),
    rafterGeometry: new THREE.BoxGeometry(slope, 0.28, 0.32),
    ridgeGeometry: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.4, 0.4, depth),
    slope,
    pitch: Math.atan2(ridge - eave, halfWidth),
  }
}

type AtticParts = ReturnType<typeof createAttic>

function applyPalette(parts: AtticParts, palette: WorldPalette) {
  paint(parts.floor, palette.floor)
  paint(parts.wall, palette.wood)
  paint(parts.beam, palette.beam)
}

function disposeAttic(parts: AtticParts) {
  for (const texture of parts.textures) texture.dispose()
  for (const material of parts.materials) material.dispose()
  for (const geometry of [
    parts.floorGeometry,
    parts.gableGeometry,
    parts.frameGeometry,
    parts.barGeometry,
    parts.roofGeometry,
    parts.kneeGeometry,
    parts.rafterGeometry,
    parts.ridgeGeometry,
  ])
    geometry.dispose()
}

export function Attic({ palette }: { palette: WorldPalette }) {
  const [parts] = useState(createAttic)
  useEffect(() => () => disposeAttic(parts), [parts])
  // Switching theme retints the attic in place.
  useLayoutEffect(() => applyPalette(parts, palette), [parts, palette])
  const { wallZ, frontZ, halfWidth, ridge, eave, windowY, rafterGap } = tuning.world.attic
  const midZ = (wallZ + frontZ) / 2
  const rafterZs: number[] = []
  for (let z = wallZ + rafterGap / 2; z < frontZ; z += rafterGap) rafterZs.push(z)

  return (
    <group>
      <mesh geometry={parts.floorGeometry} material={parts.floor} rotation-x={-Math.PI / 2} position-z={midZ} receiveShadow />

      <mesh geometry={parts.gableGeometry} material={parts.wall} position-z={wallZ} />
      <group position={[0, windowY, wallZ + 0.05]}>
        <mesh geometry={parts.frameGeometry} material={parts.beam} />
        <mesh geometry={parts.barGeometry} material={parts.beam} />
        <mesh geometry={parts.barGeometry} material={parts.beam} rotation-z={Math.PI / 2} />
      </group>

      {/* Short knee walls close the gap under the eaves. */}
      {[-1, 1].map((side) => (
        <mesh
          key={side}
          geometry={parts.kneeGeometry}
          material={parts.wall}
          position={[side * halfWidth, eave / 2, midZ]}
          rotation-y={Math.PI / 2}
        />
      ))}
      {[-1, 1].map((side) => (
        <group
          key={side}
          position={[(side * halfWidth) / 2, (ridge + eave) / 2, midZ]}
          rotation-z={-side * parts.pitch}
        >
          <mesh geometry={parts.roofGeometry} material={parts.wall} rotation-x={Math.PI / 2} />
          {rafterZs.map((z) => (
            <mesh key={z} geometry={parts.rafterGeometry} material={parts.beam} position={[0, -0.16, z - midZ]} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}
      <mesh geometry={parts.ridgeGeometry} material={parts.beam} position={[0, ridge - 0.2, midZ]} />
    </group>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Put the window and the tier in the world.** Replace `src/world/World.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { Canvas, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import type { DeviceTier } from '../logic/deviceTier'
import type { LandmarkId } from '../logic/layout'
import { tuning, type MoodId, type ThemeName } from '../tuning'
import { Attic } from './Attic'
import { Book } from './Book'
import { buildPath } from './bookPath'
import { CameraRig } from './CameraRig'
import { Dust } from './Dust'
import { paletteFor } from './palette'
import { useWorldResources } from './resources'
import { StageLighting, type ShadowState } from './StageLighting'
import { Station } from './Station'
import { WindowSky } from './WindowSky'

export interface WorldProps {
  ids: readonly LandmarkId[]
  stations: Record<LandmarkId, number>
  progressRef: { readonly current: number }
  content: Record<LandmarkId, ReactNode>
  // Saturday's moonlit night or Sunday's golden dusk (spec §3), from the invite link.
  mood: MoodId
  // Claro or Oscuro: the mood's lighter or darker look. Switching retints the scene in place.
  theme: ThemeName
  // Spec §8 Device tiers: a low device draws about half the stars, fireflies, and dust.
  tier: DeviceTier
  // False while the cover is closed: it is opaque, so the world draws the frame it needs to get
  // ready and then waits, drawing again only when something changes (a resize, new props such as
  // the ticking countdown on a page), never every frame. It runs from the moment the cover starts
  // to open.
  running: boolean
  // False until the cover has swung open: the camera waits on a wide view of the book and the pages
  // lie flat, so the guest sees the whole book before the camera swoops down to the first page.
  awake: boolean
  onReady: () => void
  onFail: (error: unknown) => void
}

type SceneProps = Omit<WorldProps, 'onFail' | 'running'>

function Scene({ ids, stations, progressRef, content, mood, theme, tier, awake, onReady }: SceneProps) {
  const palette = paletteFor(mood, theme)
  const size = useThree((state) => state.size)
  const resources = useWorldResources()
  const aspect = size.width / Math.max(1, size.height)
  const path = useMemo(
    () => buildPath(ids.map((id) => stations[id]), aspect, size.height),
    [ids, stations, aspect, size.height],
  )
  const viewRef = useRef({ progress: 0 })
  // Marked stale by a station while its paper folds, so the candle's shadow map is redrawn only then.
  const shadowRef = useRef<ShadowState>({ drawn: false, stale: true })
  const { fog, phoneMaxPx, dust } = tuning.world

  return (
    <>
      <fog attach="fog" args={[palette.fog, fog.near, fog.far]} />
      <CameraRig path={path} progressRef={progressRef} viewRef={viewRef} awake={awake} onReady={onReady} />
      <StageLighting path={path} viewRef={viewRef} resources={resources} palette={palette} shadowRef={shadowRef} />
      <Attic palette={palette} />
      <WindowSky palette={palette} tier={tier} />
      <Book resources={resources} palette={palette} />
      {ids.map((id, i) => (
        <Station
          key={id}
          id={id}
          at={stations[id]}
          anchor={path.anchors[i]!}
          viewRef={viewRef}
          awake={awake}
          resources={resources}
          shadowRef={shadowRef}
        >
          {content[id]}
        </Station>
      ))}
      <Dust count={size.width < phoneMaxPx ? dust.phone : dust.desktop} material={resources.dust} />
    </>
  )
}

// Design §2: the 3D world, loaded as its own chunk behind the HTML cover. The pages inside it are
// the landmarks' real HTML. If the renderer cannot start, the room falls back to the flat room.
export default function World({ onFail, running, ...props }: WorldProps) {
  const [createRenderer] = useState(() => (defaults: THREE.WebGLRendererParameters) => {
    try {
      return new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ ...defaults, powerPreference: 'low-power' })
    } catch (error) {
      onFail(error)
      throw error
    }
  })

  return (
    <Canvas
      className="world-canvas"
      dpr={[1, tuning.world.maxDpr]}
      frameloop={running ? 'always' : 'demand'}
      gl={createRenderer}
      flat
      shadows="percentage"
      camera={{ fov: tuning.world.fovPortrait, near: tuning.world.rig.near, far: tuning.world.rig.far }}
      onCreated={({ gl }) => {
        // The stage lighting redraws the shadow map itself, only when the paper that casts it moves.
        gl.shadowMap.autoUpdate = false
      }}
    >
      <Scene {...props} />
    </Canvas>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing prop and budget tests.** Create `src/world/props/props.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tuning, type PropName } from '../../tuning'
import { buildProps, disposeBuiltProps, propDrawCalls, propFactories, totalCalls } from './build'

describe('the lived-in attic (spec §5)', () => {
  it('builds every prop from a pure factory: the same shapes on every call', () => {
    for (const make of Object.values(propFactories)) {
      const a = make()
      const b = make()
      expect(a.parts.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
      expect(a.parts.map((p) => [p.material, p.geometry.attributes.position!.count])).toEqual(
        b.parts.map((p) => [p.material, p.geometry.attributes.position!.count]),
      )
      for (const part of [...a.parts, ...b.parts]) part.geometry.dispose()
    }
  })

  it('draws the shelf books and the yarn balls as instanced sets, each item a palette color', () => {
    const shelf = propFactories.bookshelf()
    const books = shelf.instances.find((set) => set.name === 'books')!
    expect(books.matrices).toHaveLength(tuning.world.props.books)
    const bookHexes = tuning.world.props.bookColors.map((c) => new THREE.Color(c).getHex())
    for (const color of books.colors) expect(bookHexes).toContain(color.getHex())
    const basket = propFactories.yarnBasket()
    const yarn = basket.instances.find((set) => set.name === 'yarn')!
    expect(yarn.matrices).toHaveLength(tuning.world.props.yarnBalls)
    const yarnHexes = tuning.world.props.yarnColors.map((c) => new THREE.Color(c).getHex())
    for (const color of yarn.colors) expect(yarnHexes).toContain(color.getHex())
  })

  it('lays soft contact shadows under the crib, the chair, and the trunks only (spec §8)', () => {
    const shaded = (Object.keys(propFactories) as PropName[]).filter((name) => propFactories[name]().shadow > 0)
    expect(shaded.sort()).toEqual(['crib', 'rockingChair', 'trunks'])
  })

  it('merges the static pieces of every prop into one geometry per material', () => {
    const built = buildProps()
    const keys = built.merged.map((m) => m.material)
    expect(new Set(keys).size).toBe(keys.length)
    expect(built.shadows).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(built.boxes.map((b) => b.name).sort()).toEqual((Object.keys(propFactories) as PropName[]).sort())
    disposeBuiltProps(built)
  })
})

describe('the draw-call budget (spec §8)', () => {
  const built = buildProps()
  afterAll(() => disposeBuiltProps(built))

  it('fits the props into the book and level 2 budgets, with few materials', () => {
    const calls = propDrawCalls(built)
    const { book, backdrop, bookAtRest, attic, materials } = tuning.world.budget
    expect(bookAtRest + totalCalls(calls)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(book)
    expect(attic + totalCalls(calls)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(backdrop)
    expect(calls.materials).toBeLessThanOrEqual(materials)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing placement test.** Create `src/world/props/placement.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { landmarksFor } from '../../logic/layout'
import { stationProgress } from '../../logic/path'
import { tuning } from '../../tuning'
import { buildPath } from '../bookPath'
import { buildProps, disposeBuiltProps } from './build'

const ids = landmarksFor(false)
const stationMap = stationProgress(ids)
const stations = ids.map((id) => stationMap[id])
const { cardWidth, cardPx, slotPx, lift } = tuning.world.page
const pageTall = (slotPx * cardWidth) / cardPx

// The page standing at a station (its whole slot): its four corners and its middle.
function pagePoints(anchor: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3[] {
  const bottom = anchor.y + lift
  const top = bottom + pageTall
  const half = cardWidth / 2
  return [
    new THREE.Vector3(anchor.x - half, bottom, anchor.z),
    new THREE.Vector3(anchor.x + half, bottom, anchor.z),
    new THREE.Vector3(anchor.x - half, top, anchor.z),
    new THREE.Vector3(anchor.x + half, top, anchor.z),
    new THREE.Vector3(anchor.x, (bottom + top) / 2, anchor.z),
  ]
}

const ray = new THREE.Ray()
const hit = new THREE.Vector3()

// Whether a box stands on the straight line from the camera to a point of the page.
function blocks(from: THREE.Vector3, to: THREE.Vector3, box: THREE.Box3): boolean {
  ray.origin.copy(from)
  ray.direction.subVectors(to, from)
  const length = ray.direction.length()
  ray.direction.normalize()
  return ray.intersectBox(box, hit) !== null && hit.distanceTo(from) <= length
}

const screens = [
  [390, 844],
  [360, 640],
  [1440, 900],
  [1920, 1080],
  [2560, 1440],
] as const

describe('the props never cover a page (spec §5 Placement)', () => {
  const built = buildProps()
  afterAll(() => disposeBuiltProps(built))

  it('knows a blocking box when it sees one', () => {
    const box = new THREE.Box3(new THREE.Vector3(-1, -1, 1), new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 2))
    expect(blocks(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 5), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0), box)).toBe(true)
    expect(blocks(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 5), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 3), box)).toBe(false)
  })

  it.each(screens)('stay out of the camera corridor at every station and along every glide at %i×%i', (width, height) => {
    const path = buildPath(stations, width / height, height)
    const camera = new THREE.Vector3()
    for (let step = 0; step <= 120; step++) {
      const t = step / 120
      path.camera.getPoint(t, camera)
      // The pages the camera is between: the nearest two stations.
      const near = stations
        .map((at, i) => ({ i, distance: Math.abs(at - t) }))
        .sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance)
        .slice(0, 2)
      for (const { i } of near) {
        for (const point of pagePoints(path.anchors[i]!)) {
          for (const { name, box } of built.boxes) {
            expect(blocks(camera, point, box), `${name} at t=${t.toFixed(3)}`).toBe(false)
          }
        }
      }
    }
  })
})
```

```ts
// The lived-in attic's props (spec §5).
export type PropName =
  | 'crib'
  | 'bookshelf'
  | 'yarnBasket'
  | 'rockingChair'
  | 'trunks'
  | 'lamp'
  | 'catBed'
  | 'scratchingPost'
  | 'curtains'

// Where a prop stands: [x, z] on the attic floor, turned `turn` radians about y, at `scale` times
// the size its factory draws.
export interface PropPlacement {
  at: readonly [x: number, z: number]
  turn: number
  scale: number
}
```
