- [ ] **Step 5: Write the sheet and the walk.**

Create `src/world/familySheet.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { familyFigures, rimPath, type FamilyFigure, type FigureId } from '../components/art/family'
import { tokens, tuning } from '../tuning'

// One figure's place on the sheet, in canvas pixels, and its texture coordinates.
export interface SheetCell {
  id: FigureId
  x: number
  y: number
  width: number
  height: number
  u0: number
  v0: number
  u1: number
  v1: number
}

export interface SheetLayout {
  width: number
  height: number
  pxPerUnit: number
  pad: number
  cells: SheetCell[]
}

// Spec §6 and §8: the four figures side by side on one canvas of at most sheetMaxPx a side (a power
// of two), each in its own cell with a margin for its rim of backlight. The cells sit on the canvas's
// bottom edge, so their texture coordinates start at v = 0.
export function familySheetLayout(figures: readonly FamilyFigure[] = familyFigures): SheetLayout {
  const { sheetMaxPx, sheetPad } = tuning.world.family
  const unitsWide = figures.reduce((sum, figure) => sum + figure.box.width + sheetPad * 2, 0)
  const unitsTall = Math.max(...figures.map((figure) => figure.box.height + sheetPad * 2))
  const pxPerUnit = Math.min(sheetMaxPx / unitsWide, sheetMaxPx / unitsTall)
  const width = Math.min(sheetMaxPx, THREE.MathUtils.ceilPowerOfTwo(Math.ceil(unitsWide * pxPerUnit)))
  const height = Math.min(sheetMaxPx, THREE.MathUtils.ceilPowerOfTwo(Math.ceil(unitsTall * pxPerUnit)))
  let x = 0
  const cells = figures.map((figure) => {
    const w = (figure.box.width + sheetPad * 2) * pxPerUnit
    const h = (figure.box.height + sheetPad * 2) * pxPerUnit
    const cell: SheetCell = { id: figure.id, x, y: height - h, width: w, height: h, u0: x / width, v0: 0, u1: (x + w) / width, v1: h / height }
    x += w
    return cell
  })
  return { width, height, pxPerUnit, pad: sheetPad, cells }
}

// The figures drawn from the shared SVG paths (src/components/art/family.ts), in the cats' double ink
// like the CSS family: the rim of backlight behind, then each layer's lighter offset pass, its ink
// stroke, and its fill on top, so no inner line shows.
export function familySheet(layout: SheetLayout = familySheetLayout()): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = layout.width
  canvas.height = layout.height
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('No 2D canvas for the family')
  const { line, echo } = tuning.cats
  familyFigures.forEach((figure, i) => {
    const cell = layout.cells[i]!
    ctx.save()
    ctx.translate(cell.x, cell.y)
    ctx.scale(layout.pxPerUnit, layout.pxPerUnit)
    ctx.translate(layout.pad - figure.box.x, layout.pad - figure.box.y)
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round'
    ctx.lineCap = 'round'
    ctx.globalAlpha = tuning.world.family.rimAlpha
    ctx.strokeStyle = tokens.candle
    ctx.lineWidth = tuning.family.rimWidth
    ctx.stroke(new Path2D(rimPath(figure)))
    for (const d of [figure.body, figure.hair]) {
      const path = new Path2D(d)
      ctx.globalAlpha = echo.opacity
      ctx.strokeStyle = tokens.bone
      ctx.lineWidth = echo.width
      ctx.save()
      ctx.translate(echo.dx, echo.dy)
      ctx.stroke(path)
      ctx.restore()
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1
      ctx.strokeStyle = tokens.rose
      ctx.lineWidth = line
      ctx.stroke(path)
      ctx.fillStyle = tokens.shadow
      ctx.fill(path)
    }
    ctx.restore()
  })
  const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas)
  texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace
  texture.anisotropy = 4
  return texture
}
```

Create `src/world/familyWalk.ts`:

```ts
import { familyCentreX, familyFigures, type FigureId } from '../components/art/family'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'

// Where a figure stands in the 3D doorway (the door piece's own space: x across it, z toward the
// camera), and where its walk begins.
export interface FigureSpot {
  id: FigureId
  // World units per unit of the door drawing.
  scale: number
  width: number
  height: number
  rest: readonly [x: number, z: number]
  start: readonly [x: number, z: number]
  // Seconds after the walk begins before this figure sets off.
  delay: number
}

export interface WalkPose {
  x: number
  z: number
  bob: number
  done: boolean
}

// Spec §6: the figures in the drawing's order across the door, scaled so dad is tuning.world.family.height
// tall. Each waits a step out on the page, then walks in after the one before.
export function familySpots(): FigureSpot[] {
  const { height, restZ, walkFromX, walkFromZ, staggerS } = tuning.world.family
  const tallest = Math.max(...familyFigures.map((figure) => figure.box.height))
  const scale = height / tallest
  return familyFigures.map((figure, i) => {
    const x = (figure.box.x + figure.box.width / 2 - familyCentreX) * scale
    return {
      id: figure.id,
      scale,
      width: figure.box.width * scale,
      height: figure.box.height * scale,
      rest: [x, restZ],
      start: [x + walkFromX, walkFromZ],
      delay: i * staggerS,
    }
  })
}

// Where a figure is `elapsed` seconds after the walk began: waiting at its start, walking in with a
// small bob, then resting in the doorway. Writes into `out`, so the frame loop allocates nothing.
export function walkPose(spot: FigureSpot, elapsed: number, out: WalkPose): WalkPose {
  const { walkS, bob, bobHz } = tuning.world.family
  const k = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (elapsed - spot.delay) / walkS))
  const eased = k * k * (3 - 2 * k)
  out.x = spot.start[0] + (spot.rest[0] - spot.start[0]) * eased
  out.z = spot.start[1] + (spot.rest[1] - spot.start[1]) * eased
  out.bob = k > 0 && k < 1 ? Math.abs(Math.sin(elapsed * bobHz * Math.PI)) * bob : 0
  out.done = k >= 1
  return out
}

// Two meshes per figure: the paper, and its dark edge behind.
export const familyDrawCalls = familyFigures.length * 2
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Stand the family in the doorway.** Create `src/world/FamilyCutouts.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { memo, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { tokens, tuning } from '../tuning'
import { familySheet, familySheetLayout, type SheetLayout } from './familySheet'
import { familySpots, walkPose, type FigureSpot, type WalkPose } from './familyWalk'

interface Props {
  // The Familia station's pop-up springs: the figures set off once the door stands.
  springs: RefObject<{ angle: Float32Array }>
  // The door piece's place among the springs.
  index: number
}

// A plane covering the figure's cell (the figure and its margin), with its feet on y = 0.
function figureGeometry(spot: FigureSpot, layout: SheetLayout, index: number): THREE.PlaneGeometry {
  const cell = layout.cells[index]!
  const pad = layout.pad * spot.scale
  const geometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(spot.width + pad * 2, spot.height + pad * 2)
  geometry.translate(0, spot.height / 2, 0)
  const uv = geometry.attributes.uv as THREE.BufferAttribute
  for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) {
    uv.setXY(i, cell.u0 + uv.getX(i) * (cell.u1 - cell.u0), cell.v0 + uv.getY(i) * (cell.v1 - cell.v0))
  }
  return geometry
}

function createFamily(spots: readonly FigureSpot[]) {
  const layout = familySheetLayout()
  const sheet = familySheet(layout)
  const { alphaTest } = tuning.world.family
  return {
    sheet,
    geometries: spots.map((spot, i) => figureGeometry(spot, layout, i)),
    // Unlit: the figures are silhouettes, backlit by the door's glow and their candle rim.
    paper: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: sheet, alphaTest, side: THREE.DoubleSide }),
    edge: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: sheet, alphaTest, color: tokens.shadow, side: THREE.DoubleSide }),
  }
}

type FamilyParts = ReturnType<typeof createFamily>

function disposeFamily(parts: FamilyParts) {
  parts.sheet.dispose()
  parts.paper.dispose()
  parts.edge.dispose()
  for (const geometry of parts.geometries) geometry.dispose()
}

// One scratch pose, reused every frame.
const pose: WalkPose = { x: 0, z: 0, bob: 0, done: false }

// Spec §6: the paper family as thin cutouts standing in the Familia doorway. They are children of the
// door piece, so they fold up from the page with its pop-up spring. The first time the door stands on
// a visit, they walk in once, one after another, and rest; after that they fold with the door.
export const FamilyCutouts = memo(function FamilyCutouts({ springs, index }: Props) {
  const [spots] = useState(familySpots)
  const [parts] = useState(() => createFamily(spots))
  useEffect(() => () => disposeFamily(parts), [parts])
  const figures = useRef<(THREE.Group | null)[]>([])
  const walk = useRef({ started: false, elapsed: 0, done: false })
  const { standY, edge, standAt } = tuning.world.family

  // Before the walk, every figure waits a step out on the page.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    spots.forEach((spot, i) => figures.current[i]?.position.set(spot.start[0], standY, spot.start[1]))
  }, [spots, standY])

  useFrame((_, delta) => {
    const state = walk.current
    if (state.done) return
    if (!state.started) {
      if (springs.current.angle[index]! < standAt) return
      state.started = true
    }
    state.elapsed += Math.min(delta, 0.1)
    let done = true
    for (let i = 0; i < spots.length; i++) {
      walkPose(spots[i]!, state.elapsed, pose)
      figures.current[i]?.position.set(pose.x, standY + pose.bob, pose.z)
      if (!pose.done) done = false
    }
    state.done = done
  })

  return (
    <group>
      {spots.map((spot, i) => (
        <group
          key={spot.id}
          ref={(group) => {
            figures.current[i] = group
          }}
        >
          <mesh geometry={parts.geometries[i]} material={parts.paper} />
          <mesh geometry={parts.geometries[i]} material={parts.edge} position-z={-edge} />
        </group>
      ))}
    </group>
  )
})
```

Replace `src/world/Station.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { Html } from '@react-three/drei'
import { useFrame, type ThreeEvent } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { useRef, type ReactNode, type RefObject } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import type { LandmarkId } from '../logic/layout'
import { tuning, type PieceSpec } from '../tuning'
import { FamilyCutouts } from './FamilyCutouts'
import type { WorldResources } from './resources'
import type { ShadowState } from './StageLighting'

interface Props {
  id: LandmarkId
  // The station's place along the path, 0 to 1.
  at: number
  anchor: THREE.Vector3
  viewRef: RefObject<{ progress: number }>
  // Nothing pops up until the cover opens.
  awake: boolean
  resources: WorldResources
  // The paper pieces cast the candle's shadow: moving one makes the shadow map stale.
  shadowRef: RefObject<ShadowState>
  // Tapping this station's door piece (the Familia station's) starts Misha's scratch (spec §6).
  onDoorTap?: () => void
  children: ReactNode
}

const flat = -Math.PI / 2
const { fadeFrom, fadeTo } = tuning.world.page
// Html keeps the page in the DOM when it would hide it: the text must stay reachable.
const keepVisible = () => {}

function PaperPiece({
  piece,
  resources,
  hinge,
  onTap,
  children,
}: {
  piece: PieceSpec
  resources: WorldResources
  hinge: (group: THREE.Group | null) => void
  onTap?: () => void
  children?: ReactNode
}) {
  const look = resources.pieces[piece.kind]
  const height = piece.height
  const width = height * look.aspect
  const scaleX = piece.flip ? -width : width
  const light = look.light
  const flameSize = width * tuning.world.flame.scale
  const tap = onTap
    ? (event: ThreeEvent<MouseEvent>) => {
        event.stopPropagation()
        onTap()
      }
    : undefined
  return (
    <group ref={hinge} position={[piece.x, 0, piece.z]} rotation-x={flat}>
      <mesh geometry={resources.plane} material={look.paper} scale={[scaleX, height, 1]} castShadow onClick={tap} />
      <mesh geometry={resources.plane} material={look.ink} scale={[scaleX, height, 1]} position-z={0.004} />
      {piece.kind === 'candle' && light && (
        <mesh
          geometry={resources.plane}
          material={resources.flame}
          position={[0, light[1] * height - flameSize * tuning.world.flame.sink, 0.02]}
          scale={flameSize}
        />
      )}
      {piece.kind === 'door' && (
        <mesh
          geometry={resources.plane}
          material={resources.doorGlow}
          position-z={-0.03}
          scale={[width * tuning.world.doorway.width, height * tuning.world.doorway.height, 1]}
        />
      )}
      {children}
    </group>
  )
}

// Design §1 and §2: a pop-up station. Paper cut-outs and the page itself fold up from the book as
// the camera arrives, with a springy overshoot, and lie flat again once it moves on. The page is
// the landmark's real HTML, placed in 3D, so text stays sharp and keyboards and screen readers
// reach everything. At the Familia station the paper family stands in the doorway (spec §6).
export function Station({ id, at, anchor, viewRef, awake, resources, shadowRef, onDoorTap, children }: Props) {
  const pieces: readonly PieceSpec[] = tuning.world.pieces[id]
  const doorIndex = pieces.findIndex((piece) => piece.kind === 'door')
  const { cardPx, slotPx, cardWidth, lift, zIndexRange } = tuning.world.page
  const unitsPerPx = cardWidth / cardPx
  const hinges = useRef<(THREE.Group | null)[]>([])
  const slot = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const shown = useRef(-1)
  // 0 is flat, 1 is standing. The page is the last hinge.
  const springs = useRef({ angle: new Float32Array(pieces.length + 1), velocity: new Float32Array(pieces.length + 1) })

  useFrame((_, delta) => {
    const { range, stiffness, damping, stagger, stillEps } = tuning.world.popUp
    const dt = Math.min(delta, 1 / 30)
    const target = awake && Math.abs(viewRef.current.progress - at) < range ? 1 : 0
    const { angle, velocity } = springs.current
    const count = angle.length
    let piecesMoved = false
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      // Pieces further back rise a little later.
      const k = stiffness * (1 - (stagger * i) / count)
      const before = angle[i]!
      velocity[i]! += (k * (target - before) - damping * velocity[i]!) * dt
      angle[i]! += velocity[i]! * dt
      // The page (the last hinge) casts no shadow; the paper pieces do.
      if (i < count - 1 && Math.abs(angle[i]! - before) > stillEps) piecesMoved = true
      const hinge = hinges.current[i]
      if (hinge) hinge.rotation.x = flat * (1 - angle[i]!)
    }
    if (piecesMoved) shadowRef.current.stale = true
    // A page lying flat far away would show as a sliver, so it fades out as it folds down. It stays
    // in the DOM and focusable: focus brings the camera, and the page rises again.
    const page = slot.current
    const opacity = THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(angle[count - 1]!, fadeFrom, fadeTo)
    if (page && Math.abs(opacity - shown.current) > 0.01) {
      shown.current = opacity
      page.style.opacity = String(opacity)
      page.toggleAttribute('data-flat', opacity < 0.5)
    }
  })

  const setHinge = (i: number) => (group: THREE.Group | null) => {
    hinges.current[i] = group
  }

  return (
    <group position={anchor}>
      {pieces.map((piece, i) => (
        <PaperPiece key={i} piece={piece} resources={resources} hinge={setHinge(i)} onTap={i === doorIndex ? onDoorTap : undefined}>
          {id === 'familia' && i === doorIndex && <FamilyCutouts springs={springs} index={doorIndex} />}
        </PaperPiece>
      ))}
      <group ref={setHinge(pieces.length)} rotation-x={flat}>
        <Html
          transform
          // drei draws 400 / distanceFactor CSS pixels per world unit.
          distanceFactor={unitsPerPx * 400}
          position={[0, (slotPx * unitsPerPx) / 2 + lift, 0]}
          zIndexRange={[...zIndexRange]}
          occlude={false}
          onOcclude={keepVisible}
        >
          <div ref={slot} className="station-slot" style={{ width: cardPx, height: slotPx, opacity: 0 }}>
            <section className={`landmark landmark-${id} station-page`} data-landmark-id={id}>
              {children}
            </section>
          </div>
        </Html>
      </group>
    </group>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Brighten the doorway after Cuento completo, and wire the tap.**

In `src/world/resources.ts`, replace the block from `const paper = new THREE.Color()` to the end of `applyStageMix` with:

```ts
const paper = new THREE.Color()
const candle = new THREE.Color(tokens.candle)
const bone = new THREE.Color(tokens.bone)
const unlitDoor = new THREE.Color(tokens.plum)

// Retints the shared materials for the stage and the look: the paper warms, the ink fades in, the
// flames and the doorway light up, and after Cuento completo the doorway glows brighter (spec §6).
// Runs every frame without allocating.
export function applyStageMix(r: WorldResources, mix: StageMix, palette: WorldPalette, complete: boolean) {
  paper.copy(palette.paper.sketch).lerp(palette.paper.bone, mix.ink).lerp(palette.paper.warm, mix.warm)
  for (const material of r.tinted) material.color.copy(paper)
  for (const material of r.inked) material.opacity = mix.ink
  r.flame.opacity = mix.light
  r.doorGlow.color.copy(unlitDoor).lerp(candle, mix.light)
  if (complete) r.doorGlow.color.lerp(bone, tuning.world.family.completeGlow * mix.light)
  r.dust.color.copy(palette.dust).lerp(candle, mix.warm)
}
```

Create `src/components/PaperDiorama.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render } from '@testing-library/react'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { PaperDiorama } from './PaperDiorama'

const diorama = () => document.querySelector('.paper-diorama')!
const offset = (name: string) => {
  const layer = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`.diorama-${name}`)!
  return Number(/translate3d\((-?[\d.]+)px/.exec(layer.style.transform)?.[1] ?? 0)
}

describe('the paper diorama (spec §7 Level 3)', () => {
  it("draws Saturday's attic, with stars in the window and a star garland, hidden from screen readers", () => {
    render(<PaperDiorama mood="saturday" pan={0.5} still={false} resting={false} />)
    expect(diorama()).toHaveClass('mood-saturday')
    expect(diorama()).toHaveAttribute('data-mood', 'saturday')
    expect(diorama()).toHaveAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true')
    expect(diorama().querySelectorAll('.diorama-star').length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(diorama().querySelectorAll('.diorama-garland-star').length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(diorama().querySelectorAll('.diorama-lantern')).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(diorama().querySelector('canvas')).toBeNull()
  })

  it("hangs Sunday's lanterns instead, with no stars", () => {
    render(<PaperDiorama mood="sunday" pan={0.5} still={false} resting={false} />)
    expect(diorama()).toHaveClass('mood-sunday')
    expect(diorama().querySelectorAll('.diorama-lantern').length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(diorama().querySelectorAll('.diorama-star')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('slides its layers with the flat camera, the nearer ones farther', () => {
    const view = render(<PaperDiorama mood="sunday" pan={0} still={false} resting={false} />)
    const wall = offset('wall')
    const floor = offset('floor')
    view.rerender(<PaperDiorama mood="sunday" pan={1} still={false} resting={false} />)
    expect(offset('wall')).not.toBe(wall)
    expect(Math.abs(offset('floor') - floor)).toBeGreaterThan(Math.abs(offset('wall') - wall))
  })

  it('holds still with reduced motion: no depth motion and no animation', () => {
    render(<PaperDiorama mood="saturday" pan={0.8} still resting={false} />)
    expect(diorama()).toHaveClass('is-still')
    for (const layer of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.diorama-layer')) expect(layer.style.transform).toBe('')
  })

  it('stops animating once the live attic is over it', () => {
    render(<PaperDiorama mood="saturday" pan={0.5} still={false} resting />)
    expect(diorama()).toHaveClass('is-resting')
  })
})
```

Create `src/world/backdropView.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'
import { backdropView } from './backdropView'

// Whether a point of the attic is on screen for a screen this size with the flat room panned to `pan`.
function inFrame(width: number, height: number, pan: number, point: THREE.Vector3): boolean {
  const aspect = width / height
  const view = backdropView(aspect, pan)
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(view.fov, aspect, tuning.world.backdrop.near, tuning.world.backdrop.far)
  camera.position.fromArray(view.position)
  camera.lookAt(new THREE.Vector3().fromArray(view.look))
  camera.updateMatrixWorld()
  const p = point.clone().project(camera)
  return Math.abs(p.x) <= 1 && Math.abs(p.y) <= 1 && p.z < 1
}

const { windowY, wallZ } = tuning.world.attic
const crib = tuning.world.props.placements.crib.at
const windowCentre = new THREE.Vector3(0, windowY, wallZ)
const cribTop = new THREE.Vector3(crib[0], 0.8, crib[1])

describe("level 2's camera (spec §7)", () => {
  it('frames the attic from the front, toward the window and the crib, on every screen and at every pan', () => {
    for (const [width, height] of [
      [390, 600],
      [844, 390],
      [390, 844],
      [1024, 768],
    ] as const) {
      for (const pan of [0, 0.5, 1]) {
        expect(inFrame(width, height, pan, windowCentre), `window at ${width}×${height}, pan ${pan}`).toBe(true)
        expect(inFrame(width, height, pan, cribTop), `crib at ${width}×${height}, pan ${pan}`).toBe(true)
      }
    }
  })

  it('drifts sideways with the horizontal pan, the same amount each way, and centres at the middle', () => {
    const { drift, position } = tuning.world.backdrop
    expect(backdropView(1, 0.5).position[0]).toBeCloseTo(position[0], 9)
    expect(backdropView(1, 0).position[0]).toBeCloseTo(position[0] - drift, 9)
    expect(backdropView(1, 1).position[0]).toBeCloseTo(position[0] + drift, 9)
    expect(backdropView(1, 3).position[0]).toBeCloseTo(position[0] + drift, 9)
  })

  it('turns a little as it drifts, and never moves up, down, or nearer', () => {
    const left = backdropView(1, 0)
    const right = backdropView(1, 1)
    expect(right.look[0] - right.position[0]).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(left.look[0] - left.position[0]).toBeLessThan(0)
    expect([left.position[1], left.position[2]]).toEqual([right.position[1], right.position[2]])
    expect([left.look[1], left.look[2]]).toEqual([right.look[1], right.look[2]])
  })

  it('widens its view for a portrait screen', () => {
    expect(backdropView(0.5, 0.5).fov).toBe(tuning.world.backdrop.fovPortrait)
    expect(backdropView(2, 0.5).fov).toBe(tuning.world.backdrop.fovLandscape)
  })
})
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react'
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { copy } from '../content'
import { App } from './App'

// The room stands in as a recorder of what App tells it, after each render.
const seen = vi.hoisted(() => ({ dialogOpen: [] as (boolean | undefined)[] }))
vi.mock('./WorldRoom', () => ({
  WorldRoom: function WorldRoom({ dialogOpen }: { dialogOpen?: boolean }) {
    useEffect(() => {
      seen.dialogOpen.push(dialogOpen)
    })
    return null
  },
}))

describe('the 3D pauses under a full-screen dialog (spec §8, Review Focus 4)', () => {
  it('tells the room while Tus gatos is open, and again once it closes', () => {
    render(<App search="?e=n4vk" />)
    expect(seen.dialogOpen.at(-1)).toBe(false)
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: copy.collection }))
    expect(seen.dialogOpen.at(-1)).toBe(true)
    fireEvent.keyDown(document.body, { key: 'Escape' })
    expect(seen.dialogOpen.at(-1)).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

(c) Replace the whole `vi.mock('../world/World', …)` block with:

```ts
vi.mock('../world/World', () => ({
  default: function World({
    ids,
    content,
    mood,
    theme,
    tier,
    running,
    paused,
    doorComplete,
    onDoorTap,
    onReady,
  }: {
    ids: LandmarkId[]
    content: Record<LandmarkId, ReactNode>
    mood: string
    theme: string
    tier: string
    running: boolean
    paused: boolean
    doorComplete: boolean
    onDoorTap: () => void
    onReady: () => void
  }) {
    if (world.mode === 'fails') throw new Error('The world could not start')
    useEffect(() => {
      world.mounts += 1
    }, [])
    useEffect(() => {
      if (world.mode === 'ready') onReady()
    }, [onReady])
    return createElement(
      'div',
      {
        'data-testid': 'world',
        'data-mood': mood,
        'data-theme': theme,
        'data-tier': tier,
        'data-running': String(running),
        'data-paused': String(paused),
        'data-door-complete': String(doorComplete),
      },
      createElement('canvas', { key: 'canvas' }),
      // Stands in for the 3D door piece at the Familia station.
      createElement('button', { key: 'door', type: 'button', 'data-testid': 'world-door', onClick: onDoorTap }),
      ids.map((id) => createElement('section', { key: id, 'data-landmark-id': id }, content[id])),
    )
  },
  // Level 2's live attic: it waits, gets ready, or fails on purpose; `backdrop.ready` readies it.
  Backdrop: function Backdrop({
    mood,
    theme,
    tier,
    paused,
    onReady,
  }: {
    mood: string
    theme: string
    tier: string
    paused: boolean
    onReady: () => void
  }) {
    if (backdrop.mode === 'fails') throw new Error('The attic could not start')
    useEffect(() => {
      backdrop.mounts += 1
    }, [])
    useEffect(() => {
      backdrop.ready = onReady
      if (backdrop.mode === 'ready') onReady()
    }, [onReady])
    return createElement(
      'div',
      { 'data-testid': 'backdrop', 'data-mood': mood, 'data-theme': theme, 'data-tier': tier, 'data-paused': String(paused) },
      createElement('canvas'),
    )
  },
}))
```

```ts
// jsdom's window is 1024 × 768. A test may make it shorter, or turn it, like a phone.
const jsdomWidth = window.innerWidth
const jsdomHeight = window.innerHeight
function setScreenHeight(height: number) {
  Object.defineProperty(window, 'innerHeight', { configurable: true, value: height })
}
function setScreenSize(width: number, height: number) {
  Object.defineProperty(window, 'innerWidth', { configurable: true, value: width })
  setScreenHeight(height)
}
```

```tsx
describe('three levels (spec §7)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    world.mode = 'stalls'
    world.mounts = 0
    backdrop.mode = 'waits'
    backdrop.ready = null
    backdrop.mounts = 0
    vi.mocked(hasWebGL).mockReturnValue(true)
  })
  afterEach(() => setScreenSize(jsdomWidth, jsdomHeight))

  const level = () => screen.getByRole('main').getAttribute('data-level')
  const backdropBox = () => document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.room-backdrop')
  const settleImports = () =>
    act(async () => {
      await vi.dynamicImportSettled()
    })

  it('opens the flat room over the diorama at the timeout, then fades the live attic in without moving the cards, the focus, or the pan (Review Focus 1)', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['requestAnimationFrame', 'cancelAnimationFrame', 'performance', 'setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] })
    try {
      renderWorldRoom()
      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: copy.signposts.donde }))
      act(() => {
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(tuning.world.loadTimeoutMs + 10)
      })
      expect(screen.getByTestId('room-mid')).toBeInTheDocument()
      expect(level()).toBe('diorama')
      expect(document.querySelector('.paper-diorama')).not.toHaveClass('is-resting')
      drag(surface(), [300, 400], [240, 400])
      act(() => {
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(tuning.camera.glideMs + 50)
      })
      const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: copy.map })
      act(() => link.focus())
      const card = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.landmark-donde')!
      const before = { camera: mid().style.transform, left: card.style.left, top: card.style.top }
      await settleImports()
      expect(screen.getByTestId('backdrop')).toBeInTheDocument()
      expect(backdropBox()).not.toHaveClass('is-ready')
      act(() => backdrop.ready!())
      expect(backdropBox()).toHaveClass('is-ready')
      expect(backdropBox()!.style.getPropertyValue('--backdrop-fade')).toBe(`${tuning.world.backdropFadeMs}ms`)
      expect(level()).toBe('backdrop')
      expect(document.querySelector('.paper-diorama')).toHaveClass('is-resting')
      expect(mid().style.transform).toBe(before.camera)
      expect(card.style.left).toBe(before.left)
      expect(card.style.top).toBe(before.top)
      expect(document.activeElement).toBe(link)
    } finally {
      vi.useRealTimers()
    }
  })

  it('gives a short screen the live attic behind the cards directly (spec §7)', async () => {
    setScreenSize(844, 390)
    renderWorldRoom()
    expect(screen.getByTestId('room-mid')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(document.querySelector('.book-cover')).toBeNull()
    expect(level()).toBe('diorama')
    expect(await screen.findByTestId('backdrop')).toHaveAttribute('data-mood', 'saturday')
    act(() => backdrop.ready!())
    expect(level()).toBe('backdrop')
  })

  it('drops to the diorama on the same page when the live attic loses its WebGL context, with no retry (spec §7)', async () => {
    setScreenSize(844, 390)
    backdrop.mode = 'ready'
    renderWorldRoom()
    await screen.findByTestId('backdrop')
    expect(level()).toBe('backdrop')
    const camera = mid().style.transform
    act(() => {
      screen.getByTestId('backdrop').querySelector('canvas')!.dispatchEvent(new Event('webglcontextlost', { cancelable: true }))
    })
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('backdrop')).toBeNull()
    expect(level()).toBe('diorama')
    expect(mid().style.transform).toBe(camera)
    expect(document.querySelector('.paper-diorama')).not.toHaveClass('is-resting')
    await settleImports()
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('backdrop')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('drops to the diorama when the live attic cannot start (spec §7)', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {})
    setScreenSize(844, 390)
    backdrop.mode = 'fails'
    renderWorldRoom()
    await settleImports()
    await waitFor(() => expect(backdropBox()).toBeNull())
    expect(level()).toBe('diorama')
    expect(screen.getByTestId('room-mid')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('keeps reduced motion on the still diorama: no live attic and no canvas (spec §7)', async () => {
    renderWorldRoom({ reducedMotion: true })
    await settleImports()
    expect(document.querySelector('.paper-diorama')).toHaveClass('is-still')
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('backdrop')).toBeNull()
    expect(document.querySelector('canvas')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('swaps the live attic and the diorama to the other look in place (Review Focus 2)', async () => {
    setScreenSize(844, 390)
    backdrop.mode = 'ready'
    const view = renderWorldRoom({ theme: 'dark' })
    const attic = await screen.findByTestId('backdrop')
    const diorama = document.querySelector('.paper-diorama')
    view.rerender(worldRoom({ theme: 'light' }))
    expect(screen.getByTestId('backdrop')).toBe(attic)
    expect(attic).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'light')
    expect(backdrop.mounts).toBe(1)
    expect(document.querySelector('.paper-diorama')).toBe(diorama)
    expect(diorama).toHaveAttribute('data-mood', 'saturday')
    // The diorama recolors through the head's variables: each theme has its own block for the mood.
    const { dark, light } = tuning.world.looks.saturday
    expect(dioramaCss()).toContain(`:root[data-theme="dark"] [data-mood="saturday"]{--diorama-sky-top:${cssColor(dark.skyTop)}`)
    expect(dioramaCss()).toContain(`:root[data-theme="light"] [data-mood="saturday"]{--diorama-sky-top:${cssColor(light.skyTop)}`)
  })

  it('re-frames the live attic when the phone turns, and leaves the card scale and the pan to the flat room (Review Focus 3)', async () => {
    setScreenSize(844, 390)
    backdrop.mode = 'ready'
    renderWorldRoom()
    const attic = await screen.findByTestId('backdrop')
    const before = computeLayout(844, 390, landmarksFor(false))
    const start = cameraTargetFor(before.positions.llegada, before, 844, 390)
    setScreenSize(390, 844)
    act(() => {
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'))
    })
    expect(screen.getByTestId('backdrop')).toBe(attic)
    expect(backdrop.mounts).toBe(1)
    expect(level()).toBe('backdrop')
    const after = computeLayout(390, 844, landmarksFor(false))
    const camera = clampCamera(start, after, 390, 844, tuning.camera.edgeMarginPx)
    expect(mid().style.transform).toBe(`translate3d(${-camera.x}px, ${-camera.y}px, 0)`)
    expect(document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.landmark-scale')!.style.zoom).toBe(String(after.scale))
  })
})

describe('pausing (spec §8, Review Focus 4)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    world.mounts = 0
    backdrop.mode = 'ready'
    backdrop.ready = null
    backdrop.mounts = 0
    vi.mocked(hasWebGL).mockReturnValue(true)
  })
  afterEach(() => {
    setScreenSize(jsdomWidth, jsdomHeight)
    Object.defineProperty(document, 'hidden', { configurable: true, value: false })
  })

  const hide = (hidden: boolean) => {
    Object.defineProperty(document, 'hidden', { configurable: true, value: hidden })
    act(() => {
      document.dispatchEvent(new Event('visibilitychange'))
    })
  }

  it('stops the book while a full-screen dialog is open or the tab is hidden, and resumes after', async () => {
    world.mode = 'ready'
    const view = renderWorldRoom()
    await bookReady()
    const book = () => screen.getByTestId('world')
    expect(book()).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'false')
    view.rerender(worldRoom({ dialogOpen: true }))
    expect(book()).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'true')
    view.rerender(worldRoom({ dialogOpen: false }))
    expect(book()).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'false')
    hide(true)
    expect(book()).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'true')
    hide(false)
    expect(book()).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'false')
    expect(world.mounts).toBe(1)
  })

  it('stops the live attic the same way at level 2', async () => {
    world.mode = 'stalls'
    setScreenSize(844, 390)
    const view = renderWorldRoom()
    const attic = await screen.findByTestId('backdrop')
    expect(attic).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'false')
    view.rerender(worldRoom({ dialogOpen: true }))
    expect(attic).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'true')
    view.rerender(worldRoom({ dialogOpen: false }))
    hide(true)
    expect(attic).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'true')
    hide(false)
    expect(attic).toHaveAttribute('data-paused', 'false')
    expect(backdrop.mounts).toBe(1)
  })
})
```

Create `src/world/backdropView.ts`:

```ts
import { tuning } from '../tuning'

export interface BackdropView {
  fov: number
  position: [number, number, number]
  look: [number, number, number]
}

// Spec §7 Level 2: where the live attic's camera sits for a screen of this shape (width over
// height), with the flat room panned to `pan` (0 at its left end, 1 at its right end). It slides
// sideways with the pan and turns a little the same way. Writes into `out` when given, so the frame
// loop allocates nothing.
export function backdropView(aspect: number, pan: number, out?: BackdropView): BackdropView {
  const { position, look, fovPortrait, fovLandscape, drift, yaw } = tuning.world.backdrop
  const view = out ?? { fov: 0, position: [0, 0, 0], look: [0, 0, 0] }
  const side = (Math.min(1, Math.max(0, pan)) - 0.5) * 2
  const distance = position[2] - look[2]
  view.fov = aspect < 1 ? fovPortrait : fovLandscape
  view.position[0] = position[0] + side * drift
  view.position[1] = position[1]
  view.position[2] = position[2]
  view.look[0] = look[0] + side * (drift + Math.tan(yaw) * distance)
  view.look[1] = look[1]
  view.look[2] = look[2]
  return view
}
```

Create `src/world/Backdrop.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { tierCount, tierSettings, type DeviceTier } from '../logic/deviceTier'
import { tuning, type MoodId, type ThemeName } from '../tuning'
import { Attic } from './Attic'
import { backdropView } from './backdropView'
import { createDustMaterial, Dust } from './Dust'
import { Fireflies } from './Fireflies'
import { MoodLights } from './MoodLights'
import { MoodProps } from './MoodProps'
import { paint, paletteFor } from './palette'
import { glowTexture } from './paperTextures'
import { Props } from './Props'
import { WindowSky } from './WindowSky'

export interface BackdropProps {
  mood: MoodId
  theme: ThemeName
  tier: DeviceTier
  // The flat room's horizontal position, 0 to 1, read every frame.
  panRef: { readonly current: number }
  // The tab is hidden or a full-screen dialog covers the room: nothing draws (spec §8 Pausing).
  paused: boolean
  // The first frame is drawn: the attic may fade in.
  onReady: () => void
  onFail: (error: unknown) => void
}

// Scratch values, reused every frame. There is one live attic at a time.
const target = new THREE.Vector3()
const targetLook = new THREE.Vector3()
const look = new THREE.Vector3()
const view = backdropView(1, 0.5)

// Spec §7 Level 2 camera: it eases toward its place for the screen's shape and the flat room's pan.
// A resize or a turned phone re-frames it through the new shape.
function BackdropRig({ panRef, onReady }: { panRef: { readonly current: number }; onReady: () => void }) {
  const started = useRef(false)
  useFrame((state, delta) => {
    const camera = state.camera
    backdropView(state.size.width / Math.max(1, state.size.height), panRef.current, view)
    if (camera instanceof THREE.PerspectiveCamera && camera.fov !== view.fov) {
      camera.fov = view.fov
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix()
    }
    target.fromArray(view.position)
    targetLook.fromArray(view.look)
    const k = started.current ? 1 - Math.exp(-tuning.world.backdrop.damping * Math.min(delta, 0.1)) : 1
    camera.position.lerp(target, k)
    look.lerp(targetLook, k)
    camera.lookAt(look)
    if (!started.current) {
      started.current = true
      onReady()
    }
  })
  return null
}

function disposeDust(material: THREE.PointsMaterial) {
  material.map?.dispose()
  material.dispose()
}

function BackdropScene({ mood, theme, tier, panRef, onReady }: Omit<BackdropProps, 'paused' | 'onFail'>) {
  const palette = paletteFor(mood, theme)
  const size = useThree((state) => state.size)
  const [dust] = useState(() => createDustMaterial(glowTexture()))
  useEffect(() => () => disposeDust(dust), [dust])
  useLayoutEffect(() => paint(dust, palette.dust), [dust, palette])
  const { fog, phoneMaxPx } = tuning.world
  const dustCount = tierCount(size.width < phoneMaxPx ? tuning.world.dust.phone : tuning.world.dust.desktop, tier)
  return (
    <>
      <fog attach="fog" args={[palette.fog, fog.near, fog.far]} />
      <BackdropRig panRef={panRef} onReady={onReady} />
      <MoodLights palette={palette} />
      <Attic palette={palette} />
      <WindowSky palette={palette} tier={tier} />
      <Props palette={palette} />
      <MoodProps key={mood} mood={mood} palette={palette} tier={tier} />
      {palette.motes === 'fireflies' ? <Fireflies palette={palette} tier={tier} /> : <Dust count={dustCount} material={dust} />}
    </>
  )
}

// Spec §7 Level 2: the live 3D attic behind the flat room's cards: the mood, the props, and the sky,
// with no book. It renders at backdropMaxDpr, draws at the display rate while it runs, and stops
// while paused. If its renderer cannot start, the room keeps the paper diorama.
export function Backdrop({ mood, theme, tier, panRef, paused, onReady, onFail }: BackdropProps) {
  const [createRenderer] = useState(() => (defaults: THREE.WebGLRendererParameters) => {
    try {
      return new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ ...defaults, powerPreference: 'low-power' })
    } catch (error) {
      onFail(error)
      throw error
    }
  })
  const { backdrop } = tuning.world
  return (
    <Canvas
      className="backdrop-canvas"
      dpr={[1, tierSettings(tier).backdropMaxDpr]}
      frameloop={paused ? 'never' : 'always'}
      gl={createRenderer}
      flat
      camera={{ fov: backdrop.fovPortrait, near: backdrop.near, far: backdrop.far }}
    >
      <BackdropScene mood={mood} theme={theme} tier={tier} panRef={panRef} onReady={onReady} />
    </Canvas>
  )
}
```

Replace `src/components/loadWorld.ts` with:

```ts
// The 3D book's chunk (Three.js, React Three Fiber, drei). WorldRoom loads it lazily, and
// src/main.tsx starts the same import at page start for a guest who will get the book or the live
// attic: the module cache fetches it once.
export const loadWorld = () => import('../world/World')

// Level 2's live attic (spec §7) ships in the same chunk, so a guest whose book timed out fetches
// nothing new: when the chunk arrives, the attic fades in behind the cards.
export const loadBackdrop = () => loadWorld().then((module) => ({ default: module.Backdrop }))
```

```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'

function subscribe(onChange: () => void) {
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onChange)
  return () => document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onChange)
}

// Spec §8 Pausing: whether the tab is hidden now (the guest switched apps or tabs).
export function usePageHidden(): boolean {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribe,
    () => document.hidden,
    () => false,
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the paper diorama.** Create `src/components/PaperDiorama.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useId, type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { MoodId } from '../logic/mood'
import { tuning } from '../tuning'

interface Props {
  mood: MoodId
  // The flat room's horizontal position: 0 at its left end, 1 at its right end.
  pan: number
  // Reduced motion: the same room with no depth motion (spec §7).
  still: boolean
  // The live attic (level 2) has faded in over it: it stops animating.
  resting: boolean
}

type LayerName = keyof typeof tuning.diorama.rates

// The drawing is 400 × 300 with the floor at the bottom; the round window's centre is here.
const windowX = 200
const windowY = 96

// Saturday's stars in the window, as [x, y, r] from its centre.
const stars = [
  [-22, -18, 1.6],
  [14, -26, 1.2],
  [26, -6, 1.8],
  [-8, -30, 1.1],
  [-30, 4, 1.3],
  [6, -12, 1],
  [20, 14, 1.2],
  [-16, 18, 1.4],
] as const

// The garland's or the lanterns' string, sagging between two rafters, and where things hang on it.
const stringPath = 'M30 58 Q200 118 370 58'
const hangAt = [0.08, 0.2, 0.32, 0.44, 0.56, 0.68, 0.8, 0.92]

// A point on the string above, at t from 0 to 1.
function alongString(t: number): [number, number] {
  const x = (1 - t) ** 2 * 30 + 2 * (1 - t) * t * 200 + t ** 2 * 370
  const y = (1 - t) ** 2 * 58 + 2 * (1 - t) * t * 118 + t ** 2 * 58
  return [x, y]
}

function starPoints(cx: number, cy: number, r: number): string {
  return Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => {
    const a = (Math.PI / 5) * i - Math.PI / 2
    const radius = i % 2 === 0 ? r : r * 0.45
    return `${(cx + radius * Math.cos(a)).toFixed(1)},${(cy + radius * Math.sin(a)).toFixed(1)}`
  }).join(' ')
}

function Layer({ name, pan, still, children }: { name: LayerName; pan: number; still: boolean; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { travelPx, rates } = tuning.diorama
  const style = still ? undefined : { transform: `translate3d(${Number(((0.5 - pan) * travelPx * rates[name]).toFixed(2))}px, 0, 0)` }
  return (
    <div className={`diorama-layer diorama-${name}`} style={style}>
      <svg viewBox="0 0 400 300" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMax slice" focusable="false">
        {children}
      </svg>
    </div>
  )
}

// Spec §7 Level 3: the mood's attic as layered paper cut-outs behind the flat room's cards: the back
// wall with the round window and that mood's moon or sun, the rafters, the star garland or the
// lantern string, the shelf, crib, chair, and lamp in silhouette, and the floor. The layers slide at
// different rates with the flat camera's horizontal position. Its colors are the mood and theme's,
// through the head's CSS variables (src/headMeta.ts dioramaCss). Pure DOM and SVG, no canvas.
export function PaperDiorama({ mood, pan, still, resting }: Props) {
  const id = useId()
  const sky = `${id}-sky`
  const clip = `${id}-window`
  const { windowRadius: r, twinkleMs, twinkleStaggerMs, swayMs } = tuning.diorama
  const classes = ['paper-diorama', `mood-${mood}`, still ? 'is-still' : '', resting ? 'is-resting' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
  const style = { '--diorama-twinkle': `${twinkleMs}ms`, '--diorama-sway': `${swayMs}ms` } as CSSProperties
  // The beam falls away from the moon (Saturday, on the right) or the sun (Sunday, on the left).
  const shaft = mood === 'saturday' ? 'M176 112 L224 112 L200 300 L110 300 Z' : 'M176 112 L224 112 L300 300 L210 300 Z'

  return (
    <div className={classes} data-mood={mood} aria-hidden="true" style={style}>
      <Layer name="wall" pan={pan} still={still}>
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id={sky} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
            <stop offset="0" className="diorama-sky-top" />
            <stop offset="1" className="diorama-sky-bottom" />
          </linearGradient>
          <clipPath id={clip}>
            <circle cx={windowX} cy={windowY} r={r} />
          </clipPath>
        </defs>
        <path className="diorama-wall-fill" d="M-200 300 V150 L200 18 L600 150 V300 Z" />
        <g clipPath={`url(#${clip})`}>
          <rect x={windowX - r} y={windowY - r} width={r * 2} height={r * 2} fill={`url(#${sky})`} />
          {mood === 'saturday' &&
            stars.map(([x, y, size], i) => (
              <circle
                key={i}
                className="diorama-star"
                cx={windowX + x}
                cy={windowY + y}
                r={size}
                style={{ animationDelay: `${-i * twinkleStaggerMs}ms` }}
              />
            ))}
          <g transform={`translate(${windowX} ${windowY})`}>
            <g className="diorama-orb-place">
              <circle className="diorama-halo" r={r * 1.8} />
              <circle className="diorama-orb" r={r} />
            </g>
          </g>
        </g>
        <circle className="diorama-window-frame" cx={windowX} cy={windowY} r={r} />
        <path className="diorama-window-bar" d={`M${windowX - r} ${windowY} H${windowX + r} M${windowX} ${windowY - r} V${windowY + r}`} />
        <path className="diorama-shaft" d={shaft} />
      </Layer>

      <Layer name="rafters" pan={pan} still={still}>
        <path className="diorama-rafter" d="M-150 170 L200 22 L550 170" />
        <path className="diorama-rafter" d="M60 112 H340" />
        <path className="diorama-rafter" d="M-40 300 V200 M440 300 V200" />
      </Layer>

      <Layer name="garland" pan={pan} still={still}>
        <path className="diorama-string" d={stringPath} />
        {hangAt.map((t, i) => {
          const [x, y] = alongString(t)
          return mood === 'saturday' ? (
            <polygon key={i} className="diorama-garland-star" points={starPoints(x, y + 8, 7)} />
          ) : (
            <g key={i} className="diorama-lantern-sway" style={{ animationDelay: `${-i * twinkleStaggerMs}ms` }}>
              <path className="diorama-string" d={`M${x} ${y} V${y + 8}`} />
              <ellipse className="diorama-lantern" cx={x} cy={y + 18} rx={8} ry={10} />
              <ellipse className="diorama-lantern-glow" cx={x} cy={y + 18} rx={5} ry={7} />
            </g>
          )
        })}
      </Layer>

      <Layer name="props" pan={pan} still={still}>
        <path className="diorama-prop" d="M52 262 V188 H118 V262 Z M58 212 H112 M58 237 H112" />
        <path className="diorama-prop" d="M150 262 V222 H250 V262 M150 232 H250 M162 232 V262 M176 232 V262 M190 232 V262 M204 232 V262 M218 232 V262 M232 232 V262" />
        <path className="diorama-prop" d="M292 262 L300 226 H330 L336 262 M300 226 L294 192 M298 250 Q316 270 344 252" />
        <path className="diorama-prop" d="M266 262 H282 M274 262 L270 182 L286 170 M280 172 L296 176 L290 186 Z" />
      </Layer>

      <Layer name="floor" pan={pan} still={still}>
        <rect className="diorama-floor-fill" x={-200} y={262} width={800} height={40} />
        <path className="diorama-plank" d="M-200 274 H600 M-200 287 H600 M60 262 V274 M190 274 V287 M320 262 V274" />
      </Layer>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `src/styles/diorama.css`:

```css
/* ---------- Level 3: the paper diorama (spec §7) ---------- */
/* Behind the flat room's cards. Its colors come from the mood and theme (src/headMeta.ts
   dioramaCss); nothing here is a color of its own. */
.paper-diorama {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  pointer-events: none;
  background: linear-gradient(var(--diorama-fog), var(--diorama-floor));
}

/* Wider than the screen, so sliding never shows an edge. */
.diorama-layer {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0 -12%;
  will-change: transform;
}

.diorama-layer svg {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
}

.diorama-wall-fill {
  fill: var(--diorama-wood);
}

.diorama-sky-top {
  stop-color: var(--diorama-sky-top);
}

.diorama-sky-bottom {
  stop-color: var(--diorama-sky-bottom);
}

.diorama-orb-place {
  transform: translate(var(--diorama-orb-dx), var(--diorama-orb-dy)) scale(var(--diorama-orb-scale));
}

.diorama-orb {
  fill: var(--diorama-orb);
}

.diorama-halo {
  fill: var(--diorama-halo);
  opacity: 0.3;
}

.diorama-star {
  fill: var(--diorama-sparkle);
  opacity: var(--diorama-stars);
  animation: diorama-twinkle var(--diorama-twinkle) ease-in-out infinite alternate;
}

.diorama-window-frame {
  fill: none;
  stroke: var(--diorama-beam);
  stroke-width: 6;
}

.diorama-window-bar {
  stroke: var(--diorama-beam);
  stroke-width: 3;
}

.diorama-shaft {
  fill: var(--diorama-shaft);
  opacity: var(--diorama-shaft-opacity);
}

.diorama-rafter {
  fill: none;
  stroke: var(--diorama-beam);
  stroke-width: 10;
  stroke-linecap: round;
}

.diorama-string {
  fill: none;
  stroke: var(--shadow);
  stroke-width: 1.5;
  opacity: 0.6;
}

.diorama-garland-star {
  fill: var(--diorama-sparkle);
  stroke: var(--shadow);
  stroke-width: 1;
}

.diorama-lantern {
  fill: var(--diorama-cloth);
  stroke: var(--shadow);
  stroke-width: 1;
}

.diorama-lantern-glow {
  fill: var(--candle);
  opacity: var(--diorama-lantern-glow);
}

.diorama-lantern-sway {
  transform-box: fill-box;
  transform-origin: 50% 0;
  animation: diorama-sway var(--diorama-sway) ease-in-out infinite alternate;
}

.diorama-prop {
  fill: color-mix(in oklab, var(--diorama-wood), var(--shadow) 45%);
  stroke: var(--shadow);
  stroke-width: 1.5;
  stroke-linejoin: round;
}

.diorama-floor-fill {
  fill: var(--diorama-floor);
}

.diorama-plank {
  stroke: var(--shadow);
  stroke-width: 1;
  opacity: 0.25;
}

/* Under the live attic it rests; with reduced motion nothing loops at all. */
.paper-diorama.is-resting * {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

.paper-diorama.is-still * {
  animation: none;
}

@keyframes diorama-twinkle {
  from {
    opacity: calc(var(--diorama-stars) * 0.35);
  }
  to {
    opacity: var(--diorama-stars);
  }
}

@keyframes diorama-sway {
  from {
    rotate: -4deg;
  }
  to {
    rotate: 4deg;
  }
}

/* ---------- Level 2: the live attic behind the cards (spec §7) ---------- */
/* It fades in over --backdrop-fade once its first frame is drawn; taps pass through it. */
.room-backdrop {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity var(--backdrop-fade) ease;
}

.room-backdrop.is-ready {
  opacity: 1;
}

.room-backdrop canvas {
  pointer-events: none;
}
```

Replace `src/components/WorldRoom.tsx` with:

```tsx
import {
  Component,
  lazy,
  memo,
  Suspense,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type CSSProperties,
  type ReactNode,
  type RefObject,
} from 'react'
import { copy, type CatId, type DayId } from '../content'
import { useKeyboardModality } from '../hooks/useKeyboardModality'
import { usePageHidden } from '../hooks/usePageHidden'
import { useSettledPage } from '../hooks/useSettledPage'
import { useWorldCamera } from '../hooks/useWorldCamera'
import type { BattleCat } from '../logic/battle'
import { bookQrPx, fitsBook, roomLevel, wantsBackdrop, type RoomEnvironment } from '../logic/book'
import type { CatState } from '../logic/catState'
import { deviceTier, type DeviceTier } from '../logic/deviceTier'
import { stageAt, type Stage } from '../logic/era'
import { landmarksFor, type LandmarkId, type Point } from '../logic/layout'
import { moodOfDay } from '../logic/mood'
import { isPartyOver } from '../logic/party'
import { nearestStation, stationProgress } from '../logic/path'
import { countablePages, type Progress, type ProgressEvent } from '../logic/progress'
import { hasWebGL } from '../logic/webgl'
import { tuning, type ThemeName } from '../tuning'
import { FlatRoom, type BackdropSlot, type RoomProps } from './FlatRoom'
import { loadBackdrop, loadWorld } from './loadWorld'
import { usePages } from './Pages'
import { Signposts } from './Signposts'

const World = lazy(loadWorld)
const Backdrop = lazy(loadBackdrop)

interface Props extends RoomProps {
  // Claro or Oscuro: the lighter or darker version of the day's mood (spec §3).
  theme: ThemeName
  // A full-screen dialog (the battle, Tus gatos) covers the room: the 3D stops drawing (spec §8).
  dialogOpen?: boolean
}

// Why the book gave way to the flat room (spec §7): it did not get ready in time or the screen
// turned too short (the live attic may follow), or the 3D failed (the diorama, with no retry).
export type HandoffReason = 'gaveWay' | 'failed'

// If the 3D fails anyway (the book or the live attic), the guest keeps the flat room.
class WorldBoundary extends Component<{ onError: () => void; children: ReactNode }, { failed: boolean }> {
  state = { failed: false }
  static getDerivedStateFromError() {
    return { failed: true }
  }
  componentDidCatch() {
    this.props.onError()
  }
  render() {
    return this.state.failed ? null : this.props.children
  }
}

interface PagesProps {
  ids: readonly LandmarkId[]
  stations: Record<LandmarkId, number>
  stage: Stage
  day: DayId
  now: number
  partyOver: boolean
  catState: CatState
  onGreet: (id: CatId) => void
  onChallenge: (id: BattleCat) => void
  musicPlaying: boolean
  qrUrl: string | undefined
  progress: Progress
  onEvent: (event: ProgressEvent) => void
  standing: readonly LandmarkId[]
  container: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
  noticeCamera: { readonly current: Point }
  progressRef: { readonly current: number }
  theme: ThemeName
  tier: DeviceTier
  running: boolean
  paused: boolean
  awake: boolean
  onReady: () => void
  onFail: () => void
}

// The world and its pages. Memoized, so moving the camera does not re-render the pages: the
// camera reads progress each frame instead.
const BookPages = memo(function BookPages({
  ids,
  stations,
  stage,
  day,
  now,
  partyOver,
  catState,
  onGreet,
  onChallenge,
  musicPlaying,
  qrUrl,
  progress,
  onEvent,
  standing,
  container,
  noticeCamera,
  progressRef,
  theme,
  tier,
  running,
  paused,
  awake,
  onReady,
  onFail,
}: PagesProps) {
  const { content, playAct } = usePages({
    day,
    now,
    partyOver,
    stage,
    reducedMotion: false,
    catState,
    onGreet,
    onChallenge,
    musicPlaying,
    qrUrl,
    progress,
    onEvent,
    standing,
    container,
    cameraRef: noticeCamera,
    familyInWorld: true,
  })
  // Spec §6: in the book the 3D doorway takes the frame tap, and starts Misha's scratch.
  const onDoorTap = useCallback(() => playAct('mishaScratch'), [playAct])
  return (
    <WorldBoundary onError={onFail}>
      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <World
          ids={ids}
          stations={stations}
          progressRef={progressRef}
          content={content}
          mood={moodOfDay[day]}
          theme={theme}
          tier={tier}
          running={running}
          paused={paused}
          awake={awake}
          onDoorTap={onDoorTap}
          doorComplete={progress.badges.includes('cuentoCompleto')}
          onReady={onReady}
          onFail={onFail}
        />
      </Suspense>
    </WorldBoundary>
  )
})

// Design §2 Loading: the first paint is the book cover in HTML, readable at once.
function Cover({ partyOver, opening, behind }: { partyOver: boolean; opening: boolean; behind: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`book-cover${opening ? ' is-opening' : ''}`}
      // Once the book is ready, its first page carries the same words for screen readers.
      aria-hidden={behind || undefined}
      style={{ '--cover-flip': `${tuning.world.coverFlipMs}ms` } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <div className="book-cover-inner">
        <p className="cover-title">{copy.title}</p>
        {partyOver ? (
          <>
            <p className="cover-hero">{copy.postPartyHero}</p>
            <p className="cover-hero relive">{copy.relive}</p>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p className="cover-hero">{copy.hero}</p>
        )}
        <p className="move-hint">{copy.moveHint}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function BookRoom({
  day,
  now,
  catState,
  onGreet,
  onChallenge,
  musicPlaying,
  qrUrl,
  progress,
  onEvent,
  theme,
  tier,
  paused,
  onFirstMove,
  onFail,
}: Props & { tier: DeviceTier; paused: boolean; onFail: (page: LandmarkId, reason: HandoffReason) => void }) {
  const partyOver = isPartyOver(day, now)
  const ids = useMemo(() => landmarksFor(partyOver), [partyOver])
  const pages = useMemo(() => countablePages(ids), [ids])
  const stations = useMemo(() => stationProgress(ids), [ids])
  const surface = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const keyboard = useKeyboardModality()
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false)
  const [moved, setMoved] = useState(false)
  const [coverGone, setCoverGone] = useState(false)
  const hasMoved = useRef(false)
  const firstMove = useRef(onFirstMove)

  useEffect(() => {
    firstMove.current = onFirstMove
  }, [onFirstMove])

  const openCover = useCallback(() => setMoved(true), [])
  // A drag, the wheel, or an arrow key: the guest's own move. It opens the cover and, the first
  // time, counts as the first move (design §6). Other keys and signposts only open the cover.
  // While the book is still loading behind its cover, a move counts for nothing.
  const onMove = useCallback(() => {
    openCover()
    if (hasMoved.current || !ready) return
    hasMoved.current = true
    firstMove.current?.()
  }, [openCover, ready])
  const onReady = useCallback(() => setReady(true), [])

  const cam = useWorldCamera({ stations, reducedMotion: false, onMove })
  const { glideTo, progressRef, aimRef } = cam
  const stage = stageAt(cam.progress)
  // The cover opens once the world is ready and the guest has moved, in either order.
  const opening = ready && moved

  // The book gives way to the flat room on the page the guest was on, or was heading to (spec §7):
  // it stalled or the screen turned too short (the live attic may follow), or the 3D failed.
  const handOff = useCallback(
    (reason: HandoffReason) => onFail(nearestStation(aimRef.current, stations), reason),
    [aimRef, onFail, stations],
  )
  const giveWay = useCallback(() => handOff('gaveWay'), [handOff])
  const failHard = useCallback(() => handOff('failed'), [handOff])

  // Design §6: a page is found once the camera rests within tuning.world.stationEps of its
  // station (the cover never counts). Nothing is found, or stands, until the cover opens: before
  // that the guest sees only the cover.
  const atStation = opening
    ? pages.find((id) => Math.abs(cam.progress - stations[id]) <= tuning.world.stationEps)
    : undefined
  useSettledPage(atStation, (page) => {
    if (!progress.pages.includes(page)) onEvent({ type: 'reachedPage', page, pages })
  })

  // The pages standing now: within the pop-up range of the camera, the same range that stands a
  // page up. Their objects open at once, found or not (the facts are never gated). Keyed by the ids,
  // so the memoized pages re-render only when the set changes.
  const standingKey = opening
    ? ids.filter((id) => Math.abs(cam.progress - stations[id]) <= tuning.world.popUp.range).join(',')
    : ''
  const standing = useMemo(() => (standingKey ? (standingKey.split(',') as LandmarkId[]) : []), [standingKey])

  // Any key opens the cover, so a keyboard never tabs into a closed book.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (moved) return
    window.addEventListener('keydown', openCover)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', openCover)
  }, [moved, openCover])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!opening) return
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setCoverGone(true), tuning.world.coverFlipMs)
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [opening])

  // Facts are never gated: a world that stalls before its first frame gives way to the flat room.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) return
    const timer = window.setTimeout(giveWay, tuning.world.loadTimeoutMs)
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [ready, giveWay])

  // Mobile browsers drop WebGL contexts, most often while the guest is away in WhatsApp after
  // Confirmar. The event does not bubble, so the surface catches it on its way down to the canvas.
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = surface.current
    if (!el) return
    const onLost = () => failHard()
    el.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost, true)
    return () => el.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost, true)
  }, [failHard])

  // Design §9: a screen that turns too short for a readable page (a phone turned sideways) gives
  // way to the flat room for the rest of the visit.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onResize = () => {
      if (!fitsBook(window.innerHeight)) giveWay()
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize)
  }, [giveWay])

  // Keyboard focus inside a page glides the camera to that page (BR-20, FR-11). The pages live in
  // their own DOM roots inside the surface, so this listens on the DOM directly.
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = surface.current
    if (!el) return
    const onFocusIn = (e: FocusEvent) => {
      if (!keyboard.current || !(e.target instanceof Element)) return
      const id = e.target.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-landmark-id]')?.dataset.landmarkId as LandmarkId | undefined
      const at = id === undefined ? undefined : stations[id]
      if (id && at !== undefined && Math.abs(progressRef.current - at) > tuning.world.stationEps) glideTo(id)
    }
    // The browser may scroll a clipped box to show focus. The camera owns position, so undo that.
    const onScroll = (e: Event) => {
      if (!(e.target instanceof HTMLElement)) return
      e.target.scrollLeft = 0
      e.target.scrollTop = 0
    }
    el.addEventListener('focusin', onFocusIn)
    el.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, true)
    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener('focusin', onFocusIn)
      el.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll, true)
    }
  }, [glideTo, keyboard, progressRef, stations])

  // Noticing measures the cats' real positions on screen. For rushing, the camera moves one
  // pixel per pixel of finger drag, as in the flat room.
  const noticeCamera = useMemo(
    () => ({
      get current(): Point {
        return { x: (progressRef.current * window.innerWidth) / tuning.world.dragProgressPerViewport, y: 0 }
      },
    }),
    [progressRef],
  )

  const goTo = (id: LandmarkId) => {
    openCover()
    glideTo(id)
  }

  return (
    <main className="world-room" data-era={stage} aria-label={copy.a11y.room}>
      <div
        ref={surface}
        className="world-surface"
        // The QR is drawn larger in the book, so it stays at its minimum size on a page at its smallest.
        style={{ '--qr-px': `${bookQrPx}px` } as CSSProperties}
        {...cam.surfaceProps}
      >
        <BookPages
          ids={ids}
          stations={stations}
          stage={stage}
          day={day}
          now={now}
          partyOver={partyOver}
          catState={catState}
          onGreet={onGreet}
          onChallenge={onChallenge}
          musicPlaying={musicPlaying}
          qrUrl={qrUrl}
          progress={progress}
          onEvent={onEvent}
          standing={standing}
          container={surface}
          noticeCamera={noticeCamera}
          progressRef={progressRef}
          theme={theme}
          tier={tier}
          running={opening}
          paused={paused}
          awake={coverGone}
          onReady={onReady}
          onFail={failHard}
        />
        {!coverGone && <Cover partyOver={partyOver} opening={opening} behind={ready} />}
      </div>
      <Signposts landmarks={ids} onGo={goTo} />
    </main>
  )
}

// Levels 2 and 3 (spec §7): the flat room's cards over the paper diorama, with the live attic fading
// in behind them where it can run. The flat room stays mounted through every change here, so its
// camera, focus, and cards never move: a lost context or an error only takes the attic away, for the
// rest of the visit.
function FlatLevels({
  room,
  startAt,
  env,
  tier,
  paused,
}: {
  room: Props
  startAt: LandmarkId | undefined
  env: Omit<RoomEnvironment, 'backdropReady'>
  tier: DeviceTier
  paused: boolean
}) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false)
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false)
  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const current = { ...env, failed: env.failed || failed }
  const wants = wantsBackdrop(current)
  const shown = roomLevel({ ...current, backdropReady: ready }) === 'backdrop'
  const fail = useCallback(() => setFailed(true), [])
  const onReady = useCallback(() => setReady(true), [])

  // A lost WebGL context behind the cards leaves the diorama. The event does not bubble, so the box
  // catches it on its way down to the canvas.
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = box.current
    if (!wants || !el) return
    el.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', fail, true)
    return () => el.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', fail, true)
  }, [wants, fail])

  const mood = moodOfDay[room.day]
  const backdrop: BackdropSlot | undefined = wants
    ? (pan) => (
        <div
          ref={box}
          className={`room-backdrop${shown ? ' is-ready' : ''}`}
          aria-hidden="true"
          style={{ '--backdrop-fade': `${tuning.world.backdropFadeMs}ms` } as CSSProperties}
        >
          <WorldBoundary onError={fail}>
            <Suspense fallback={null}>
              <Backdrop mood={mood} theme={room.theme} tier={tier} panRef={pan} paused={paused} onReady={onReady} onFail={fail} />
            </Suspense>
          </WorldBoundary>
        </div>
      )
    : undefined

  return (
    <FlatRoom
      startAt={startAt}
      day={room.day}
      now={room.now}
      reducedMotion={room.reducedMotion}
      catState={room.catState}
      onGreet={room.onGreet}
      onChallenge={room.onChallenge}
      musicPlaying={room.musicPlaying}
      qrUrl={room.qrUrl}
      progress={room.progress}
      onEvent={room.onEvent}
      onFirstMove={room.onFirstMove}
      backdrop={backdrop}
      backdropShown={shown}
    />
  )
}

// Spec §7: three levels. The full 3D book (a day link, motion allowed, WebGL, a screen tall enough,
// and ready in time); else the flat room's cards over the live attic where WebGL can run; else over
// the paper diorama. Once the book gives way, the flat room stays for the rest of the visit, opening
// on the page the guest was on.
export function WorldRoom(props: Props) {
  const [handoff, setHandoff] = useState<{ page: LandmarkId; reason: HandoffReason } | null>(null)
  // The screen's height at start. From then on the book itself watches it.
  const [height] = useState(() => window.innerHeight)
  // Spec §8 Device tiers: read once per visit.
  const [tier] = useState(() => deviceTier(navigator))
  const hidden = usePageHidden()
  // Spec §8 Pausing: nothing 3D draws while the tab is hidden or a full-screen dialog covers the room.
  const paused = hidden || Boolean(props.dialogOpen)
  const onFail = useCallback((page: LandmarkId, reason: HandoffReason) => setHandoff({ page, reason }), [])
  const env = {
    reducedMotion: props.reducedMotion,
    height,
    webgl: hasWebGL,
    bookGaveWay: handoff !== null,
    failed: handoff?.reason === 'failed',
  }
  if (roomLevel({ ...env, backdropReady: false }) === 'book') {
    return <BookRoom {...props} tier={tier} paused={paused} onFail={onFail} />
  }
  return <FlatLevels room={props} startAt={handoff?.page} env={env} tier={tier} paused={paused} />
}
```

In `src/components/App.tsx`, add `dialogOpen={collectionOpen || battleCat !== null}` to the `<WorldRoom …>` element after `theme={theme}`.

In `src/logic/book.ts`, replace `shouldPreloadWorld` and its comment with:

```ts
// Design §2 Loading and spec §7: a guest on a day link who will get the book or the live attic starts
// fetching the World chunk at page start, alongside the entry script, instead of once the room has
// rendered.
export function shouldPreloadWorld(search: string, env: BookEnvironment): boolean {
  return (
    resolveInvite(search).kind === 'day' &&
    roomLevel({ ...env, bookGaveWay: false, failed: false, backdropReady: true }) !== 'diorama'
  )
}
```

```ts
// room starts fetching the World chunk now, before the first render. If this fetch fails, the room's
// own lazy load fails too and leaves the paper diorama.
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the canvas settings.** Create `src/world/canvasSettings.ts`:

```ts
import { tierSettings, type DeviceTier } from '../logic/deviceTier'

export interface CanvasSettings {
  dpr: [number, number]
  shadows: 'percentage' | false
}

// Spec §8 Device tiers: the book's canvas. A low device gets no real shadow map and a lower pixel ratio.
export function bookCanvas(tier: DeviceTier): CanvasSettings {
  const settings = tierSettings(tier)
  return { dpr: [1, settings.maxDpr], shadows: settings.shadows ? 'percentage' : false }
}

// Level 2's live attic never has a shadow map (it has no candles); its pixel ratio is lower still.
export function backdropCanvas(tier: DeviceTier): CanvasSettings {
  return { dpr: [1, tierSettings(tier).backdropMaxDpr], shadows: false }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Apply the tier to the canvases, and add the dev-only readout.**

Create `src/world/DrawCallReadout.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber'
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'

// Spec §8: a dev-only readout of the draw calls per frame (renderer.info.render.calls), in a corner
// of the screen. World and Backdrop mount it only when import.meta.env.DEV is true, so production
// builds drop it. It reads the last frame's count every 30 frames.
export function DrawCallReadout({ label }: { label: string }) {
  const out = useRef<HTMLOutputElement | null>(null)
  const frames = useRef(0)

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = document.createElement('output')
    el.className = 'draw-calls'
    document.body.append(el)
    out.current = el
    return () => {
      el.remove()
      out.current = null
    }
  }, [])

  useFrame((state) => {
    frames.current += 1
    const el = out.current
    if (!el || frames.current % 30 !== 0) return
    el.textContent = `${label}: ${state.gl.info.render.calls} calls`
  })

  return null
}
```

```js
async (page) => {
  const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page)
  await cdp.send('Network.enable')
  await cdp.send('Network.emulateNetworkConditions', { offline: false, latency: 562.5, downloadThroughput: 188743, uploadThroughput: 86400 })
  await cdp.send('Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate', { rate: 4 })
  const failed = []
  page.on('requestfailed', (request) => failed.push(request.url()))
  const started = Date.now()
  await page.goto('http://localhost:4173/?e=n4vk', { waitUntil: 'commit' })
  await page.locator('.cover-title').first().waitFor({ state: 'visible' })
  const coverMs = Date.now() - started
  await page.locator('main.room, main.world-room').first().waitFor({ state: 'attached', timeout: 15000 })
  const firstRoom = await page.locator('main').first().getAttribute('class')
  const firstLevel = await page.locator('main').first().getAttribute('data-level')
  const backdropArrived = firstRoom === 'room'
    ? await page.locator('.room-backdrop.is-ready').waitFor({ timeout: 60000 }).then(() => true, () => false)
    : null
  await cdp.send('Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate', { rate: 1 })
  return { coverMs, firstRoom, firstLevel, backdropArrived, failed }
}
```

(b) Insert before the line `### When the party is over`:

```md
### The moods

The invite link picks the mood, and the theme picks its lighter (Claro) or darker (Oscuro) version, so there are four looks. The clock never picks a mood; after the party it stays the same.

| | Claro | Oscuro |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Saturday (`?e=n4vk`): Noche de luna** | Blue hour: a deep blue sky with a rising moon; the attic reads clearly. | Full night: a big moon with a halo, twinkling stars, and cool moonlight tinted toward Misha's blue. |
| **Sunday (`?e=q8lm`): Atardecer dorado** | Golden afternoon: a warm sun in the window, and candle-gold light on the floor. | Late dusk: the sun on the horizon, a sky going from candle and rose to plum, the lanterns lit, and fireflies out. |

Each look sets the sky behind the round window, the moon or sun and its halo, the window's light, the ambient light, the fog, and the tints of the wood, the floor, and the beams. Every color is a mix of the palette tokens and the feeling colors (section 16).

- **Saturday's own props:** a star-and-crescent mobile turning slowly over the crib, a paper star garland along the rafters, a soft moonbeam from the window, and stars twinkling outside it.
- **Sunday's own props:** a string of paper lanterns, swaying gently and glowing, fireflies drifting in the air at dusk, and a golden beam from the window.
- **Motion:** at most two ambient motions at once: the mobile or the lanterns, plus the dust or the fireflies. The family's walk-in is the one orchestrated moment.
- **Lit from the start:** the attic, its sky, and its light follow the mood on every page. Only the book's pop-up paper and pieces, and the cats, go from pencil (boceto) to ink (tinta) to light (luz); the candles still light up in luz.

### The lived-in attic

Both moods share one attic, furnished in the pink-noir paper style (flat shapes, ink edges, slightly crooked): a crib under the round window, where the mood's mobile hangs; a bookshelf with colored books; a yarn basket; a rocking chair; three stacked old trunks; a crooked floor lamp, lit by its own glow in the Claro looks only; a cat bed; a scratching post; and curtains at the window. Soft painted shadows lie under the crib, the chair, and the trunks. No cobwebs, graves, or anything sad.

The props stand along the walls, behind and around the book. None stands between the camera and a page, at a page or while gliding between pages, and none covers a page or the HUD. Behind the flat room's cards (level 2) they frame the view.

```

They are told apart by height and hair, far enough apart that the hair reads:

In the flat room they are layered paper in the card's own door, and tapping the door frame starts Misha's scratch.

(e) Replace the three bullets **Short screens.**, **Claro.**, and **Facts are never gated.** (lines 330–332) with:

```md
- **Short screens.** A screen shorter than 640 px (a phone held sideways, the smallest phones with their browser toolbars) cannot show the tallest page whole at that size, so it gets the flat room's cards over the live attic (level 2, see *Three levels*). The height is checked when the page loads and whenever the screen is resized: a book that turns too short hands over to the flat room, on the page the guest was on, for the rest of the visit.
- **Moods.** The attic takes the day's mood and the theme's look (see *The moods*), lit from the first page. Switching the theme swaps the look in place.
- **Facts are never gated.** If the book cannot start, fails, has not drawn its first frame after 6 seconds, or loses its WebGL context, the flat room takes over (see *Three levels*).
- **Pausing.** The book and the live attic stop drawing while the tab is hidden or a full-screen dialog (the battle, **Tus gatos**) is open, and resume afterwards.
```
